murukaṉ allatu aḻaku

nūṉmukam
iyaṟki pala aḻakiya vaṭivaṅkaḷāyk kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.  akkāṭciyi maṟuppōrilli.  iyaṟkiyik kūrtta mati
yāl ārāya ārāya ataṉ uḷḷuṟiyoṉṟu uṇarvil uṟukiṟatu.  iyaṟki uṭal;  ataṉ uḷḷuṟi uyir.  uyiri muruku allatu murukaṉ eṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭār koṇṭaṉar.  ivvuṭaliyum uyiriyum ulakam ārāyntavaṇṇamiruk
kiṟatu.  avaravar ārāycciyiṟ pōnta uṇmikaḷum palappala.  iṉṉum ārāycci vaḷarntu varukiṟatu.
murukaṉippaṟṟiya palatiṟak koḷkikaḷ, katikaḷ, kaṭṭukaḷ nāṭṭil ulavukiṉṟaṉa.  mutal mutal murukaṉuṇmi kaṇṭa paḻantamiḻar, murukaṉi evvāṟu koṇṭaṉar eṉṟu ārāyat talippaṭṭatil, avar muruki, maṇamum, iḷami
yum kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyum aḻakumuṭiya cemporuḷākak koṇṭaṉar eṉpatum piṟavum viḷaṅkiṉa.  ikkoḷki aṭiyēṉukku mika viḻumiyatāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu.  ivviḻumiya koḷki nāṭṭil nilipeṟiṉ, nāṭṭukkup palavaḻiyilum nalaṉ viḷiyumeṉṉum vēṭki eṉmāṭṭeḻuntatu.  vēṭki mēlīṭṭāṉ, amayam vāyntuḻi vāyntuḻi, viḻumiya koḷkiyip paṟṟip pēciyum eḻutiyum varukiṟēṉ.
kārikkuṭiyiṉiṉṟum mukiḻtta kumaraṉ eṉṉun tiṅkaḷ malariṉ mutalitaḻil, eṉṉāl eḻutappeṟṟa murukaṉ eṉṉuñ ciṟu kaṭṭuri yoṉṟu veḷivantatu.  akkaṭ
ṭuriyik kaṇṭa naṇpar cilar, ati nūl vaṭivāka veḷiyiṭu
māṟu, kaṭitam eḻutikkoṇṭē yiruntaṉar.  akkaṭṭuri oru tiṅkaḷ tāḷukkeṉa eḻutappaṭṭamiyāṉ, ati yāṉ niṉinta vaṇṇam eḻutal iyalāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu.  aṉparkaḷ viḻintavāṟu, piṉṉi, akkaṭṭuriyi mutaṉ muṟi nūlākka muyaṉṟapōtum, ataṉiṭi, niṉinta pala poruḷ
kaḷ cērkkap pōtiya ōyvu kiṭikkavilli.  ākavē mutaṟ patippil mikac cila poruḷ cērttu, nūlukku murukaṉ eṉṉun talippīntēṉ.  appatippu 1925-m āṇṭu veḷi
vantatu.
aḻakippaṟṟi eṉpāl karukkoṇṭirunta pala poruḷi iraṇṭām patippil urukkoḷḷac ceyyalāmeṉṟu eṇṇik koṇṭiruntēṉ.  avveṇṇam iraṇṭām patippil oruvāṟu muṟṟuppeṟṟatu.  iraṇṭām patippu 
1927-m āṇṭu veḷivaralāyiṟṟu.
iraṇṭām patippil pala putupporuḷ kūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.  nūliṉ uḷḷuṟikkēṟpa nūlukku murukaṉ allatu aḻaku eṉṉun talippu aṇiyappaṭṭatu.  aḻaki aṭippaṭiyāk kiṭatti nūl eḻutappaṭṭamiyāṉ, nūlukku ammuṭi cūṭṭap paṭṭateṉka.
iyaṟkiyaḻaki murukeṉakkoṇṭu paḻantamiḻar vaḻipāṭu nikaḻtti vantaṉar.  ivvāṟu murukaṉi vaḻipaṭuvōr toki nāḷaṭivil arukiviṭṭatu.  ataṉāl pala kaṭavuḷar uṇarvu, paṉṉeṟi uṇarvu, pōrāṭṭam, kaṭcikaḷ, pirivukaḷ mutaliya iḻivukaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa.  paḻiya iyaṟki vaḻipāṭu mīṇṭum puttuyir peṟiṉ, iḻivukaḷ tolital orutali.  iyaṟki vaḻipāṭu mīṇṭum nāṭṭil uyirtteḻal  vēṇṭu
meṉpatu eṉatu vēṇavā.  avvavā mēlīṭṭāṉ yākkappaṭṭa nūl itu.
innūṟkaṇ, murukaṉ coṟporuḷ viḷakkam, aḻaki
ṉiyal, iḷamip pēṟu, iyaṟki yaḻakē murukeṉpatu, pāṭṭu ōviyam ici ivikaḷiṉ vaḻi iyaṟki aṉṉiyi vaḻi
paṭal, peṇṇiṉ perumi, nōṉpiṉ tiṟam, murukaṉaṭiyār iyalpu mutaliyaṉa ōtappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  innūli muṟṟum payilvōr uḷḷattil iyaṟki vaḻipāṭṭil ārvam, orē kaṭavuḷ orē neṟi eṉṉum uṟuti, evvuyirkkun tīṅku niṉiyāp pēraṟam mutaliyaṉa nilavum.
iyaṟki eṉṉum eṉatu āruyir aṉṉikkum, av
viyaṟkiyi viṭuttu eṉṟum nīṅkāta aḻaku eṉṉum appa
ṉukkum innūlik kōyilākkit, tamiḻ malar tūvi, ollum vaki vaḻipāṭu nikaḻtti yirukkiṟēṉ.  vaḻipāṭṭu muṟiyil kuṟṟaṅ kuṟikaḷiruppiṉ, poṟuttaruḷumāṟu aṉparkaḷi vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.
mūṉṟām patippu 1930-m āṇṭilum, nāṉkām patippu 1938-m āṇṭilum, aintām patippu 1943-m āṇṭilum, āṟām patippu 1944-m āṇṭilum, ēḻām patippu 1946-m āṇṭilum veḷivantaṉa.  iḵtu eṭṭām patippu.  
mutumiyil, eṉ kaṇkaḷil ūṟu nērntatu.  cikicci ceyyappaṭum ivvēḷiyil, ivveṭṭām patippu ac
cākiyatu.  eṉatu nili kaṇṭu, accup piḻi pārttut tuṇi
purinta vittuvāṉ - aṉpu kaṇapati avarkaṭku eṉatu naṉṟi uriyatāka.
piḻi poṟukka.

kumarakuruparar piḷḷittamiḻ
ulaku kuḷira ematu matiyil oḻuku mamuta kiraṇamē
	uruku maṭiya ritaya nekiḻa uṇarvi leḻuna lutayamē
kaliyu niṟiyu maṟivu mutira mutiru matura naṟavamē
	kaḻuvu tukaḷar muḻuka neṭiya karuṇi peruku calatiyē
alakil puvaṉa muṭiyum veḷiyil aḷiyu moḷiyi ṉilayamē
	aṟivu ḷaṟivi yaṟiyu mavarum aṟiya variya piramamē
maliyiṉ makaḷkaṇ maṇiyi yaṉiya matali varuka varukavē
	vaḷami taḻuvu pariti puriyiṉ maruvu kumaraṉ varukavē
iḻume ṉaruvi coriyu mimiya mutalvi putalvaṉ varukavē
	iyalu naṭiyum vaṭivu maḻakum eḻuta variyaṉ varukavē
oḻuku karuṇi muḻuku kamala vataṉaṉ varuka varukavē
	oruva ṉiruva roṭuki toḻunal upaya caraṇaṉ 	varukavē
viḻutu viṭuve ṇilavu poḻiyu nakiyaṉ varuka varukavē
	viḷari payilu maḷiyu ñimiṟum viravu kuravaṉ varukavē
maḻali mutira mutiru matura vacaṉaṉ varuka varukavē
	maḻali mutira mutiru matura vacaṉaṉ varuka varukavē
vaḷami taḻuvu pariti puriyiṉ maruvu kumaraṉ varukavē

1. murukaṉ poruḷum potumiyum
(murukaṉ - muruku - murukiṉ coṟporuḷ - maṇam iḷami kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi aḻaku - paḻantamiḻar kūrttamati - murukaṉ oru kūṭṭattavar kaṭavuḷ allaṉ - moḻi vēṟṟumiyāl iṟivaṉ pala peyar peṟal - maṇam iḷami iṟimi aḻaku ellārkkum potu.)
murukaṉ evaṉ?  murukiyuṭiyavaṉ murukaṉ.  muruku eṉṟāl eṉṉi,  muruku eṉpatu pala poruḷ kuṟikkum  oru col.  ap pala poruḷuḷ ciṟappākak kuṟikkattakkaṉa nāṉku.  avi maṇam, iḷami, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi, aḻaku eṉpaṉa.  innāṉku poruḷaṭaṅkiya oru collāl paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ muḻumutaṟ poruḷi aḻittatu viyakkattakkatu.  iyaṟki maṇamum, māṟā iḷamiyum, ellāp poruḷiyuṅ kaṭantoḷirun taṉmiyum, aḻiyā aḻakum iṟivaṉiṭattil ilaṅkuvatu kaṇṭu, apporuḷkaḷ muṟiyē uṟitaṟ kiṭam
peṟṟuḷḷa murukaṉ eṉṉuñ colli,  avviṟivaṉukkup paḻan tamiḻmakkaḷ cūṭṭiyataṉ tiṟamiyi nōkkuḻi avarkaḷatu kūrttamati pulaṉākiṟatu.
ulakattiṉ nāṉā pakkaṅkaḷilum vāḻntu varum makkaḷ, taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ aṟivāṟṟaluk kēṟṟavāṟu, taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ moḻiyil kaṭavuḷukkup palatiṟap peyarkaḷ cūṭṭiyiruk
kiṟārkaḷ.  appeyarkaḷ ovvoṉṟiṉum ovvoru ciṟappup poruḷ viḷaṅkukiṟatu.  cilavaṟṟil poruttamillāp poruḷkaḷum uṇṭu.  murukaṉ eṉṉuñ collilō aṟiñar pōṟṟum poruḷkaḷē miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.  ātalāl aṟiñar pōṟṟaṟkuriyatum, makiḻcci yūṭṭakkūṭiyatum, porutta muṭiyatumāka iruppatu murukaṉ eṉṉun tiruppeyar.
murukaṉ oru kūṭṭattavarkkuriya kaṭavuḷ eṉṟu karutuvatu aṟivuṭimi yākātu.  ek kūṭṭattavar emmoḻiyāl pōṟ
ṟum epporuḷāyiṉum, apporuṭkaṇ iṟi iyalpukaḷ kāṇappeṟiṉ, atik kōṭalē aṟivuṭimi yākum.  iyaṟki maṇamum, māṟā iḷamiyum, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyum, aḻiyā aḻakum aṟiviṟ ciṟanta eccamayattavaruṅ koḷḷum āṇṭavaṉukkuriya iyalpukaḷām.  ivviyalpukaḷ aminta oṉṟit tamiḻmakkaḷ murukaṉ eṉṟu vaḻuttukiṟārkaḷ.  ēṉiya moḻiyiṉarum avviyalpukaḷiṉ poruḷ nalka valla tamatu moḻiyāl tām vaḻipaṭum iṟivaṉukkup pala peya
riṭṭaḻikkalām.  appeyarp poruḷi ārāyntu, tamiḻil peyartteḻutiṉāl, atu murukāka viḷaṅkutal vēṇṭum.  tamiḻp peyarākiya murukeṉuñ col taṉakkuriya poruṇmi aḻiyappeṟātu emmoḻiyil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭāleṉṉa?  kaṭavuḷi maṇamuṭiyatākavum, iḷamiyuṭiyatākavum, iṟimiyuṭiyatākavum, aḻakuṭiyatākavum evar em
moḻip peyarāl toḻiṉum, āṇṭu emperumāṉ murukaṉ eḻuntaruḷvāṉeṉka.
maṇamum iḷamiyum kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyum aḻakum evaruṭimi?  ivi ellārkkumuriya uṭimiyallavō?  ātalāl maṇamuṭimi, iḷamiyuṭimi, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi
yuṭimi, aḻakuṭimi ākiya ivikaḷip pōṟṟa maṟup
pōr murukaṉip pōṟṟa maṟuppōrāvar.  maṇamuṭimi mutaliya nāṉkiṉiyum pōṟṟa maṟuppōr iruppu neñcamum vaṉkaṇmiyumuṭiya muraṭarāyiruppar.  aṉṉār neñcil muruku alartalum aritu.

2.  aḻakiṉ iyalum kūṟum
(aḻaku - aḻiyā aḻaku - aḻiyum aḻaku - nakkīraṉārum aḻakum - iyaṟki ceyaṟki - uḷporuḷ ilporuḷ - parumi nuṇmi - aḻakarumpumiṭaṅkaḷ - aḻakukkum iyaṟkikkumuḷḷa toṭarpu - aḻakiṉ paṉmi orumi - aḻaku puriyun tuṇi - aḻakum pulaṉkaḷum - puṟa aḻaku aka aḻaku - aḻakukkut tōṟṟa oṭukkamiṉmi - pēriṉpam ciṟṟiṉpam - vaṉṟoṇṭar māṇikka
vācakar - aḻakum iṉpamum - akanāṉūṟu, kalittoki, pattup
pāṭṭu, tirukkōviyār - peṇṇum aḻakum - aḻakuk kōyilkaḷ - aḻakum vāḻvum - aḻakiṭimaṇam iḷami kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi maruval - aḻaku peṟum vaḻi - uṇavu muṟi - perun
tiṇṭi kaḷiyāṭal maruntukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kēṭukaḷ - mēlnāṭ
ṭār iyaṟki muṟi - kantaḻi - pāṇṭi nāṭṭil kaṭavuḷi aḻakāk koṇṭatu - tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyil murukiṉ nāṉku kūṟu.)
iṉi maṇam, iḷami, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi, aḻaku eṉṉum nāṉku poruḷil aḻaku eṉpati maṭṭum yāṉ īṇṭuc ciṟappāka eṭuttuk koḷkiṟēṉ.  eṉṉi?  aḻaku uḷḷa iṭattil ēṉiya mūṉṟum viravi niṟṟaliṉ eṉka.  aḻiyā aḻakil maṇamum iḷamiyum iṟimiyum kalantu niṟṟal iyalpu.
aḻiyā aḻaku eṉṟu īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭṭatu uṉṉaṟ
pālatu.  aḻiyā aḻakeṉil aḻiyum aḻakeṉṟu oṉṟuṇṭō eṉṉum aiyam cilaruḷḷattilātal piṟakkalām.  aḻakiṉ aḻiyāmi nōkki, īṇṭu aḻiyā eṉṉum aṭiyāl aḻakic ciṟappittataṉṟi, aḻiyum aḻakukku etirmaṟiyāka aḻiyā eṉṟu aḻakic ciṟappikkavilli yeṉṟuṇarka.
ulakil ovvōruṇmikkum māṟupaṭṭa pōliyuṇṭu.  pōlikkaṇ uṇmi yiṉmiyāṉ, aḵtu aḻintupaṭukiṟatu.  immuṟiyil aḻiyā uṇmi yaḻakiṟkum māṟupaṭṭa aḻiyum pōli yaḻakeṉṟu oṉṟirukku maṉṟō?  appōli aḻaki aḻiyum aḻaku eṉṟu aḻikkalāmaṉṟō?
itaṉāl aḻaki irukūṟupaṭutti aḻiyā aḻaku.  aḻiyum aḻaku eṉṟu collavēṇṭuvatilli.  aḻivati aḻakeṉṟuṅ koḷḷa vēṇṭuvatilli.  itu paṟṟiyē aḻiyā eṉṟu aḻakic ciṟappittavāṟu kāṇka.  aḻakup pōliyi aḻiyum aḻaku eṉṟatum mukamaṉ eṉka.
iyaṟkiyōṭu vāḻntu, iyaṟkiyōṭu paṭintu, iyaṟkiyi āyntu, iyaṟkiyi luṟiyum aḻakeṉum murukik kaṇṭa paḻantamiḻp pulavarkaḷ aḻakiṉ iyalpip paṟṟi eṉṉa kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ? atic caṟṟu nōkkuvōm.
paḻan tamiḻp pulavarkaḷuḷ īṇṭu nakkīraṉār oruvarik kōṭal cālpu.  nakkīraṉāriṉ pulami karuti maṭṭum avari īṇṭukkoḷḷa vēṇṭuvatilli.  avar iyaṟkiyil muruki yuṇarntu murukāṟṟuppaṭi pāṭiyatil tamatu pulami celuttiyavar eṉṉun toṭarpurimi koṇṭu avari īṇṭuk kōṭalē ēṟpuṭittu.  nakkīraṉār tāmuṇarnta aḻakip potu muṟiyil tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyākap pāṭi makiḻntār.  avvāṟṟup paṭikkaṇ ōriṭattil aḻakiṉ iyalpu ittakiyateṉac ciṟappu muṟiyil avar aṟivuṟutti
yirukkiṟār.  kipuṉin tiyaṟṟāk kaviṉpeṟu vaṉappu eṉṟu appulavar aḻakiyal terittavāṟu kāṇka.
kiyāl ceyyappaṭuvatu ceyaṟki.  kiyāl ceyyap
paṭātatu iyaṟki.  maliyum āṟum kāṭum kaṭalum evar kiyāl ākkappaṭṭaṉa?  ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum viṇmīṉkaḷum evarāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa?  puṉalukkut taṇmi īntavar evar?  neruppukku vemmi yūṭṭiṉavar yāvar?  iyalpāka arumpiya ivikaḷiṉ aḻakē kipuṉin tiyaṟṟāk kaviṉpeṟu vaṉappākum.  maṟṟiyatu pōli.
kipuṉin tiyaṟṟā ivvaḻaku aḻiyāt taṉmiyatu eṉṟu mēlē tokiyākac collappaṭṭatu.  ati īṇṭu vakippaṭuttic cila uri pakartal nalaṉeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu.  aḻaku uḷporuḷā allatu makkaḷ uḷḷam iyaṟkiyil paṭiyumpōtu aṅkē uruveḷiyāyt tōṉṟum il
poruḷā eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu.
aḻakip paṟṟi eṇṇāta iṭamilli;  pēcāta iṭamilli;  pōṟṟāta iṭamilli.  aḻakiṉmāṭṭu ulakaṅ
koṇṭuḷḷa paṟṟuppōla vēṟetaṉ māṭṭum aḵtu ap
paṟṟuk koṇṭilli.  aḻaku aḻaku eṉṟē ulakam aḻakil uṟintu niṟkiṟatu.  ivvāṟu aḻakuṭaṉ vāḻum ulaki nōkki, aḻaku eppaṭi irukkiṟatu eṉṟu viṉaviṉ, atic collāl colla iyalātu atu viḻikkiṟatu.  pulaṉ
kaḷukkum poruḷākāta oṉṟippaṟṟi eṉṉa colvatu,  eṉṉē aḻakiṉ nuṭpam!
pulaṉkaṭkup poruḷākāmi eṉṉum oṉṟu koṇṭu, aḻakiṟkiṉmi kūṟalāmō eṉiṉ, aḵtum aṟa
meṉat tōṉṟavilli.  eṉṉi?  uṇarviluṟuttal, viṉi
yāṟṟal, tuṉpa nīkki iṉpamākkal mutaliyaṉa aḻakāl peṟu
kiṟōm.  ittaki nikaḻvuṭiya oṉṟaṉ iruppi eṅṅaṉam maṟuttal kūṭum?
ulakil pulaṉkaḷukkup poruḷākum poruḷkaḷu
muṇṭu;  poruḷākāp poruḷkaḷumuṇṭu.  ivikaḷi muṟiyē parumi (Concrete) eṉṟum nuṇmi (Abstract) eṉṟum ulakaṅ koṇṭirukkiṟatu.  parumikku uṇmi kūṟalum, nuṇmikku iṉmi kūṟalum aṟivuṭimiyalla.  eṉṉi?
ūṉap pulaṉkaḷukkup poruḷākātu uṇarvil nikaḻcci
yaḷavil uṟuttum poruḷkaḷumuṇṭu.  aṟivu aṟam aṉpu nīti mutaliyaṉa pulaṉkaḷukku vaṭivāl kāṭci vaḻaṅkum parup poruṭkaḷalla.  ivi kāṭci vaḻaṅkāmiyāṉ, ivaṟṟiṉ uṇmikkum maṟuppuk kūṟalām pōlum!  ivi uḷḷaṉavē yaṉṟi illaṉa valla.  ivaṟṟiṉ uṇmi, ivaṟṟi yuṭiya parupporuḷ vāyilāka uṇarak kiṭakkiṟatu.  aṟiñaṉ vaḻi aṟivum, aṟavōṉ vaḻi aṟamum, aṉpaṉ vaḻi aṉpum, nīti
māṉ vaḻi nītiyum uṇarviṟpaṭutal ōraṟpāṟṟu.  ivipōṉṟa pala nuṇmikaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟuḷ aḻakumoṉṟu.  aḻakuṭip poruḷ koṇṭu aḻaki yuṇarkiṟōm.  ākavē, aḻakeṉpatu parup pulaṉkaḷukku vaṭivākat tōṉṟāta nuṇṇiya uḷporuḷeṉak koḷka.  aḵtu uruveḷi yaṉṟeṉat teḷika.
annuṇmip poruḷ yāṇṭum nīkkamaṟa niṟpatu.  yāṇṭum niṟintuḷḷa annuṇporuḷ cila vēḷikaḷil cila iṭaṅkaḷil taṉatiruppi uyirkaṭku aṟivuṟuttavō eṉṉavō miṉṉoḷipōl taṉ (nuṇṇiya) tōṟṟattip pulappaṭuttukiṟatu.  iḷañāyiṟu taṉatu ceṅkatiri nīlakkaṭalil parappumpōtu apparaviyiṭi aḻaku oḷirkiṟatu.  añ
ñāyiṟu taṉatu iḷaveyili, pacuṅkaṭal poṅki yeḻuntā
leṉa uruṇṭu tiraṇṭu parantu oḷi nuḻitaṟku miṭamiṉṟic ceṟintu miṭintu carintu cāyntu niṟkuṅ kuṟiñcik kāṭukaḷiṉ pacci mēṉiyil umiḻumpōtu avviṭi aḻaku arumpu
kiṟatu.  tiṅkaḷ taṉ pāl nilavi, veḷḷi veḷēleṉa veḷḷi aṟal paṭarntāleṉa miḷirum veḷḷiya maṇalmītu kālumpōtu aṅkē aḻaku oḻukukiṟatu.  ivvāṟu cila vēḷikaḷil cila iṭaṅkaḷil aḻaku taṉatu nuṇṇiya kāṭci vaḻaṅkutal uṇarka.
aḻaku etaṉ vāyilāka uṇarak kiṭakkiṟatu?  aḻaku taṉṉiyuṭiya iyaṟki vāyilāka uṇarak kiṭakkiṟatu.  ataṟkum itaṟkumuḷḷa toṭarpeṉṉi?  aḻakukkum iyaṟkikkumuḷḷa toṭarpi eṉṉeṉṟu  kūṟuvatu?  aḻakiṉ uṭal iyaṟki.  iyaṟyiṉūṭē aḻaku nīkkamiṉṟi virāvi niṟkiṟatu.  iyaṟkiyi viṭuttu aḻaki ārāyn
tuṇaral aritu.  aḻakiṉ iruppuṇartaṟkuk karuviyāyuḷḷa iyaṟkiyum aḻakippōla uḷporuḷēyām.  iyaṟki, palap pala vaṭivaṅkaḷākat tōṉṟit tōṉṟi maṟiyiṉum, ataṉ mutal kēṭuṟuvatilli.  uḷḷataṟkut tōṟṟamē yaṉṟi illataṟkut tōṟṟamilli eṉpatu aḷavi.  aḻiyātu kiṭakkum iyaṟki mutalukku aṭippaṭiyāka nilavuvatu aḻakākum.  aḻakiṉiṉṟum arumpi malarntu kāṭciyaḷippatu iyaṟki eṉṟu curuṅkac collalām.  aḻakukkum iyaṟkikku
muḷḷa toṭarpu nōkki, aḵtē iḵtu iḵtē aḵtu eṉṟuṅ koḷḷalām.
taṉakku aṭippaṭiyāka niṟkum aḻakip palappala vaṭivaṅkaḷāka iyaṟki kāṭṭik kāṭṭi, ulakik kavarntu makiḻcci yūṭṭukiṟatu.  ovvōr iyaṟkip poruṭkaṇ karutti oṟṟi oṟṟi, uṉṉa uṉṉa ataṉataṉ aḻaku taṉit
taṉiyē uṇarvil uṟutal peṟalām.  ippayiṟci mutira mutira ellā iyaṟkip poruṭkum aṭippaṭiyāka orē aḻaku nilaval uṇaralām.  eṉavē aḻaku taṉittaṉip paṉmiyākavum, paranta orumiyākavum uyirkaṭku uṟutuṇi ceytal ōrka.
aḻaku uyirkaṭkup purintuvarūum uṟutuṇiyi eṉ
ṉeṉ ṟurippatu?  aḻakō, pulaṉkaṭkup poruḷākātatu.  aḵtu uyirkaṭkup purintuvarūun tuṇiyō, aḷavil aṭaṅkātatu.  aḻaku pulaṉkaṭkup poruḷākiṟatilli.  āṉāl atu pulaṉkaṭku viruntākiṟatu.  aḻakil tōyāta pulaṉkaḷ uramiḻantu ularntu pōkum.  pulaṉkeṭṭa uyir
kaṭku vāḻvētu?  vaḷarcci yētu?  iṉpamētu?  iṉpa nukarc
cikku vāyākavuḷḷa pulaṉkaḷ aḻakil paṭinta vaṇṇamiruttal vēṇṭum.  appaṭivāl pulaṉkaḷ parukum viruntamiḻtam  uṭaliyum uyiriyum ōmpuvatākum.
aḻaku puṟattē niṟpatupōla akattēyum niṟkiṟatu.  puṟamum akamum oṉṟumpōtu aḻakuṇarvu pulaṉākum.  ataṟkuk karuvikaḷāka iruppaṉavē pulaṉkaḷ.  appulaṉkaḷ aḻakil tōyntatum, āṅkāṅkuḷḷa aḻaku, malariṉiṉṟum maṇam vīcuvatupōlat, taṉṉiṉiṉṟum taṉ oḷi poḻiyat toṭaṅkukiṟatu.  ataṉāl uṭalum uyirum ākkamuṟum.  aḻaku pulaṉākāta nuṇmiyatāy ulakiṟkup purintuvarūum utavi uṇarvāl uṇarattakkatu.
ulakiṟku aṭippaṭiyāy niṉṟu tuṇipuriyum aḻaku, tōṟṟa voṭukkamuṭiyatāyiṉ, ati aḻaku eṉṟu aḻit
tal poruntātu.  tōṟṟa oṭukka muṭiya māṟutali (vikā
ratti) aḻaku eṉṟu eṅṅaṉam aḻippatu?  tōṟṟa voṭukka
milā oṉṟē aḻakākum.  attakiya oṉṟip paṭippavar yāvar?  vaḷarppavar yāvar,  ikkaruttuppaṟṟiyē aḻaki aḻiyā iyaṟki aḻaku eṉṟu āṉṟōr āṇṭu varukiṉṟār.  avvaḻakil tōyntu aḻakarāya nakkīraṉār, aḻakik, kippuṉin tiyaṟṟāk kaviṉperuvaṉappu eṉṟu kūṟippōn
tār.  ivvaḻakē paḻantamiḻar pōṟṟiya muruku murukākum.
kipuṉin tiyaṟṟā iyaṟki aḻakil karuttip patiya
vittuḷḷa oruvaṉ, ataṉōṭu puṇaruntoṟum puṇarun
toṟum avaṉuḷḷattuḷ colaṟkariya iṉpaṅ kiḷarnteḻum.  avviṉpattip piṉvantār pēriṉpa meṉavum ciṟṟiṉpa meṉavum pirittuk kūṟiṉār.  paṇṭināḷil iṉpam iṉpa
mākavē piriviṉṟik kiṭantatu.  makkaḷ vāḻvu, ceyaṟkiyil vīḻnta nāḷ  toṭṭu, iṉpam pēriṉpameṉavum, ciṟṟiṉpa meṉavum pirintatu pōlum!  paṇṭit tamiḻ nūlkaḷ aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉṉum mūṉṟi maṭṭum aṟivuṟuttali iṉṟuṅ kāṇalām.  makkaḷ ceyaṟki vāḻvil talippaṭṭa nāḷtoṭṭu vīṭṭiṉpameṉa oru taṉiyiṉpam vakukkappaṭṭatu pōlum!  muṉṉi nāḷil nāṉkāvatākiya vīṭṭiṉpa meṉum pēriṉpam mūṉṟāvatākiya iṉpattil aṭaṅkiyiruntatu.  ātalāl iyaṟki yaḻakil tōyum oruvaṉatuḷḷam nukarum iṉpam ciṟṟiṉpamāmō pēriṉpamāmō eṉṟu ārāya vēṇṭuvatilli.  ati muṉṉiya muṟippaṭi iṉpameṉṟu moḻiyalām; piṉṉiya vaḻakkuppaṭi pēriṉpameṉac collalām.  avviṉpattukku oppākavātal uyarvākavātal vēṟōriṉpamuḷatō?  avviṉpatti yaṉṟō vaṉṟoṇṭar nukarntār?  māṇikkavācakar parukiṉār?
avviṉpam aḻakuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilellāmirukkum.  aḻakillā iṭamumuṇṭō?  ivvulakamē aḻakuvaṭivam.  ivvaḻakiya ulakiṭi niṉṟu aṇṇāntu pārttāl, mēlum kīḻum cuṟṟum muṟṟum aḻaku corivatu kāṇalām.  aḻakiya nīlap paṭāttiṉ virivil nittilan tūviṉāleṉa vāṉatti
lūruñ cevviya aḻaki eṉṉeṉṟu varuṇippatu?  ñāyiṟ
ṟiṉ oḷiyi eṉṉeṉṟu navilvatu?  tiṅkaḷiṉ nilavi eṉṉeṉṟu ceppuvatu?  kōḷkaḷiṉ miṉṉali eṉṉeṉṟu kūṟuvatu?  kariya kāṭṭiṉ kāṭciyi eppaṭi eṭuttuk kāṭṭu
vatu?  kinīṭṭum alikaṭaliṉ kaviṉi eṅṅaṉam kaḻaṟuvatu?  eṉṉē aṇṭattiṉ aḻaku!  iviyelāñ cērnta oṉṟē iyaṟki yaṉṉiyiṉ aḻaku vaṭivam.  avvaḻakē aḻiyā aḻaku.  atuvē murukeṉṉum pēraḻaku.  
paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ, iyaṟkivaḻi vāḻvu celutti, ataṟku aṭippaṭiyāyuḷḷa aḻakuṇmi kaṇṭu, avvaḻakāl iyaṟki aḻakupeṟuvatu nōkki, avviyaṟkiyi vaḻipaṭṭu, muḻumutaṟporuṭṭu aḻaku eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa murukaṉ eṉṉum peyaraṇintārkaḷ.  avarkaḷ iyaṟki vāyilāka murukik kaṇṭu nukarnta iṉpatti akanāṉūṟu, kalit
toki, pattuppāṭṭu, tirukkōviyār mutaliya nūlkaḷil kāṇalām.  avviṉpa ūṟṟit tiṟakkavalla aḻaku iyaṟki vāyilākak kāṇakkiṭakkiṟatu.  iyaṟkiyil uḷḷattip patittu, ataṉōṭu oṉṟi vāḻntuvariṉ, iyaṟkip pēraḻaku - muruku - pulaṉākum.
cempoṉṉi yurukki vārttāleṉak kāṭciyaḷikkum antivāṉ cekkaraḻakum, koṇṭal koṇṭalāka ōṭum puyaliṉ aḻakum, atu poḻiyum maḻiyiṉ aḻakum, at taṇpuṉal maṇaṟ kaṟkaḷi arittōṭum aruviyiṉ aḻakum, paccip pacēleṉap peruṅkāṭci yaḷikkum poḻilkaḷiṉ aḻakum, avikaḷil paccip pāmpeṉap piṉṉik kiṭakkum pacuṅkoṭikaḷiṉ aḻakum, avikaḷiṉiṉṟum arumpiyuḷḷa nakimalariṉ aḻakum makkaḷ uḷḷattuḷḷa iṉpa ūṟṟit tiṟappaṉa vallavō?  mayilāṭum aḻakum, māṉ naṭakkum aḻakum, nantūrum aḻakum pulavarukku viruntallavō?
ivvaḻakelān tiraṇṭa aḻakē peṇṇaḻaku.  peṇ, mātar eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷukku aḻakeṉpatē poruḷ.  palatiṟa iyaṟki aḻakukaḷellām avaḷ māṭṭiruttalāl, avaḷi aḻakeṉum peṇ - mātar - eṉṟu nantamiḻ makkaḷ aḻit
tārkaḷ.  aḻakukku uṟiviṭamākiya peṇṇiṉpāl etu illi? ellāmirukkiṉṟaṉa.  avaḷ māṭṭu mukiluṇṭu;  tiṅkaḷuṇṭu;  malarkaḷuṇṭu;  kuyiluṇṭu;  mayiluṇṭu;  māṉuṇṭu;  piṟavum uṇṭu.  murukaṉ vaṭivelām avaḷ.  avaḷ pāl - muruki - aḻakik kāṇṭalaṉṟō iṉpam?
ittakiya iṉpap poruḷit tuṉpap poruḷāka evaṉ koḷvāṉ?  kāmukaṉ - kīḻmakaṉ - kayavaṉ koḷvāṉ.  iyaṟki yiṉpa nuṭpan terinta oruvaṉ orunāḷum peṇṇit tuṉpapporuḷākak karutāṉ.  peṇṇiṉal
lāḷ pāṭṭāka irukkiṟāḷ;  enti murukaṉāka irukkiṟāḷ;  tūya iṉpamāka irukkiṟāḷ.  appeṇ teyvattit toḻukiṟēṉ.  peṇṇeṉum teyva aḻaku, aṟiyāmi aḻukkāṟu avā mutaliya koṭumip pāṟikaḷi uṭittu, iṉpa ūṟṟit tiṟakkum.  avviṉpattil tōynta paḻan
tamiḻp pulavar pāṭalkaḷ murukaṉ uṟiyum aḻakuk kōyil
kaḷām.  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷip paṭippatum aḻakeṉum murukaṉi vaḻipaṭuvatākum.
murukaṉ iyaṟki vāyilākat taṉataḻaki nāṭōṟum poḻiyātiruppiṉ ulakil aḻakētu?  aḻakilliyēl vāḻvētu?  oruvaṉatu vāḻvu avaṉatu aḻakiyē poṟuttu niṟkiṟatu.  uṭalil narappil uraṅkuṉṟap peṟukiṟavaṉ aḻa
kuṭiyavaṉāyirāṉ.  oruvaṉatu uṭal nalatti avaṉatu aḻaku pulappaṭuttum.  ulakiluḷḷa uyirkaḷellām uṭal nalamuṟṟu iṉpan tuyttu vāḻtal vēṇṭum eṉṉum pēraruḷāl murukaṉ iyaṟki vāyilāka aḻaki utavukiṟāṉ.  emperu
māṉ nīlakkaṭalil kāliyil neruppup piḻampupōla ñāyi
ṟākap puṟappaṭṭu ulakuk kutavāviṭiṉ, ulakam eṉṉa pāṭu
paṭum?  avaṉ tiṅkaḷāka amutam poḻintu ulakuk kiṉpa mūṭṭuvati uṇarāta pulaṉkaḷum pulaṉkaḷāmō?  murukaṉ maliyāka niṉṟum, āṟāka ōṭiyum, kāṭāka viḷaṅkiyum, kaṭa
lāka olittum, uṭalāka uṭaṉiruntum uyirkaḷi ōmpivarum tiruvaruṭṭiṟatti evarē pukaḻavallār?  murukaṉ iyaṟki vāyilāka āṭivarum tiruviḷiyāṭalkaḷi īṇṭu virikkiṟ perukum.
iyaṟki vāyilāka murukaṉ purintuvarum pērutaviyi makkaḷ nērē peṟuvārkaḷāyiṉ, avarkaḷ eṉṟum aḻiyā aḻakuṭiyavarkaḷā yiruppārkaḷ.  iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭa vāḻvil makkaḷ talippaṭalāl, avarkaḷ virivil aḻaku kuṉṟi māṇṭuviṭukiṟārkaḷ.  ātalāl makkaḷ iyaṟkikku araṇāmāṟu vāḻvu naṭāttap payilvārkaḷāka.
iyaṟki vāḻvu naṭāttuvōr uḷḷam, aḻukkāṟu avā mutaliya yama tūtarkaḷ ulavum nilamākātu, avarkaḷ uḷḷam aṉpu irakkam mutaliya teyva kaṇaṅkaḷ vāḻum naṉṉilamākum.  aḻukkāṟu avā mutaliya pēyttaṉmikaḷ makaṉi nōy vāyppaṭutti virivil avaṉik kollum.  aviyallā aṉpu irakkam mutaliya teyva nīrmikaḷ oruva
ṉatu vāḻvi vaḷarkkum.  itaṟkuriya vaḻi iyaṟki yaḻakām muruki - murukaṉi - vaḻipaṭuvatākum.  eṉṟum iḷiyāy aḻakiyāy eṉṟu murukaṉatu iyalpi ōtiya nakkīraṉār, uṉṉi yoḻiya oruvariyum nampukilēṉ - piṉṉi yoruvariyāṉ piṉcellēṉ  eṉṟu aruḷic ceytirut
tal kāṇka.  eṉṟum aḻiyā aḻaki virumpuvōr emperu
māṉ murukaṉaṭiyit tamakkuriya pukalāk kōṭal vēṇṭum.
iyaṟki vāyilāka muruku eṉṉum aḻiyā aḻaki uṇarntavarpāl eṉṟum maṇamē kamaḻntu koṇṭirukkum;  avarmītu pulāl maṇam kamaḻātu.  avar maṇattukkeṉa vēṟu ceyaṟkip poruḷit tēṭa vēṇṭuvatilli.
vikaṟit tuyileḻuntu, kālik kaṭaṉi oḻuṅkāka muṭittu, taṇ puṉalāṭi, naṟuṅ kāṟṟil mūḻki, ñāyiṟṟoḷiyiṟ paṭintu, iyaṟki uṇavukoṇṭu puṟatti aḻaku ceytum, aḻakkāṟu vekuḷi avā mutaliya tī nīrmikaḷik kaḷintu, aṉpu aruḷ aṟam mutaliya naṉṉīrmikaḷi maṇantu, akatti aḻaku ceytum vāḻum oruvaṉmītu iyaṟki maṇaṅ kamaḻumeṉpatu orutali.  ivvāḻvu peṟāta cōmpariṉ puṟattilum akattilum aḻukal nāṟṟam vīcum.  iyaṟki vāḻvu naṭāttum oruvaṉatu uḷḷum puṟamum murukeṉum aḻaku kōyil koḷkiṟatu.  aḻakuḷḷa iṭattil pulāl muṭikku iṭamētu?  aḻakil maṇamiruttal iyalpu.
iyaṟki maṇaṅ kamaḻap peṟuvōr eṉṟum iḷami
yuṭiyarā yiruppar.  iyaṟki maṇam uṭal nalaṉukkuk kēṭu cūḻātu.  makkaḷi mutumi yaṇukātavāṟu ammaṇaṅ kāttu
varum.  aḻakil maṇam oṉṟi niṟṟalpōla ataṉkaṇ iḷami
yum oṉṟi niṟkiṟatu.  iḷami virumpātār ulakil uḷarō?  akavi mutirnta kiḻavaṉum mīṇṭum iḷami peṟa virumpuvaṉ.  iḷamiyil makkaḷukkuḷḷa kātalukkō raḷavilli.  iḷami, murukaṉ kūṟukaḷiloṉṟu.  ākavē eṉṟum iḷamiyuṭiyava
rāka vāḻa virumpuvōr emperumāṉ murukaṉi eppoḻu
tum niṉinta vaṇṇamiruppārāka.
iyaṟkikku mutalāka (kāraṇamāka) uḷḷa muruku eṉṟum iḷamiyā yiruppatāl, iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷ taṅkaḷ kaṭamikaḷic cevvaṉē āṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.  murukiṉ iḷami kuṉṟuvatāyiṉ, iyaṟkiyum taṉ ceyalil kuṉṟum.  āṉāl  iyaṟki ettuṇik kālam evvev vaḻiyil toḻiṟ paṭiṉum, aḵtu iḷittuc calittuc cāyvatilli.  eṉṉi?  mutalākavuḷḷa muruku eṉṟum iḷamiyāyiruppatāl, viṉiyākiya iyaṟkiyum eṉṟum iḷami eṉṉum vaḷami kuṉṟāmal, oru peṟṟiyāyt taṉ ceyalic calippiṉṟic cāyviṉṟi naṭāttikkoṇṭu pōkiṟatu.  ātalāl iyaṟki
vaḻi muruki yuṇarvōṉ eṉṟum iḷiyaṉāyiruppaṉ.
malikkiḻaṅki uṇṭum, kāṭṭup paḻaṅkaḷit tiṉṟum, vayal maṇikaḷi (tāṉiyaṅkaḷi)p pucittum, kaṭaṟ kāṟṟip parukiyum vāḻvōri virivil mutumi aṇukātu.  iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷit taṅkaḷ viruppappaṭi vēṟu pala poruḷoṭum pulāloṭuṅ kalantu avittup pataṉceytu vilāp puṭikka uṇpatilēyē poḻutu pōkkum mākkaḷi virivil mutumi aṭarntu varuttum.  perun tiṇṭiyāṉum, kaḷiyāṭalkaḷāṉum, tīyoḻukkattāṉum uṭal vaḷattik keṭuttu, mutumiyeṉṉum mūtēviyi maṇantu koṇṭa cilar, mīṇṭum iḷami yāñ cītēviyi maṇakka muyalkiṟār.  immuyaṟciyil talippaṭuvōruṭ cilar poṉ veḷḷi irumpu ivikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa maruntukaḷi uṇkiṟār;  cilar naṭip payiṟci kutiri yēṟṟap payiṟci mutaliya tuṟi
kaḷiliṟaṅkukiṟār;  cilar paṭukkiyōṭu kiṭantu, kaṭavuḷē kaṭavuḷē eṉṟu vāyāl maṭṭuṅ kaṭavuḷic collik koṇṭirukkiṟār.  amerikkā, jermaṉi mutaliya iṭaṅ
kaḷil mutumi nīkki iḷamipeṟa iḷaṅkuraṅkukaḷiṉ uyirppu muḷikaḷip peyarttu makkaḷpāl avikaḷi amikkum muṟi kiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.  ōruyir nalattukku iṉṉōr uyirik kolvatu iyaṟkic cenneṟiyākātu.  iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭu vāḻvit toṭakkattil naṭāttip piṉṉic cākkāṭṭukkañci maruttuvam mutaliya ceyaṟki muṟikaḷik kiyāḷvatiṉum, muṉṉiya piḻipāṭuṇarntu iyaṟki aṉṉiyiṉ tiruvaruḷippeṟa, mīṇṭum avaḷ aruḷ nāṭiyuḻippiṉ avaḷ iraṅkiyaruḷvāḷ.
iyaṟkit tuṇiyāl mutiyōr, iḷamip pēṟu peṟutal aritaṉṟu.  amerikkāvil cila aṟiñar iyaṟkit tuṇiyāl iḷami kākka muyaṉṟu varukiṟār.  paṇṭit tamiḻnāṭṭār iyaṟkiyōṭu muraṇpaṭā vāḻvu naṭātti, iyaṟki aḻakām murukaṉi vaḻipaṭṭamiyāṉ, avar iḷami iṉpam nukarntuvantaṉar.  kumaraṉi maṉa moḻi meykaḷāl toḻuvōr eṉṟuṅ kumaraṉāyiruppatu iyalpē.  iḷami kākka virumpuvōr eṉṟum iḷiyaṉāka uḷḷa murukaṉip poruḷ terintu pōṟṟi uyvārāka.
iyaṟki maṇamum māṟā iḷamiyum uṭiya oṉṟu evvita māṟutalum eytātu.  aḵtu eṉṟum oru peṟṟiyatā yilaṅkum.  māṟupāṭiṉṟi eṉṟum oru peṟṟiyatā yiruppatu kaṭavuḷiyalpākum.  tōṟṟam oṭukkameṉṉum māṟutal tattuvattukkuṇṭu.  māṟutalillāk kaṭavuḷ, tattuvaṅ kaṭanta taṉipporuḷeṉṟu aṟivu nūlkaḷ muḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.  ituvē kantaḻi eṉpatu.  iyaṟki maṇamum māṟā iḷamiyum 
māṟutaluṭiya tattuvattukkilli.  avi māṟutalillāk kaṭavuḷukkēyuṇṭu.  aḻiyā aḻakil iyaṟki maṇamum, māṟā iḷamiyum iruttalpōlak kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyu miruttal kāṇka.  ivi yelāmuṭiya oṉṟi murukaṉ eṉṟu nam muṉṉōr koṇṭaṉar.  ammurukaṉi iṭiyīṭiṉṟi vaḻi
paṭṭu variṉ kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyum peṟalām.
ākavē muruku eṉṉum aḻakil maṇamum iḷamiyuṅ kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyum oṉṟiyiruttaluṇarka.
paḻan tamiḻar tām toḻuta kaṭavuḷi aḻakup poru
ḷākavē koṇṭaṉar  eṉpataṟkup pala cāṉṟukaḷ uḷa.  ac
cāṉṟukaḷuḷ iraṇṭoṉṟu varumāṟu :
tamiḻ piṟanta iṭamum, tamiḻ vaḷartta kuṟumuṉi vāḻnta iṭamum, tamiḻc caṅkam maruviya iṭamum pāṇṭi nāṭu eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu.  appāṇṭi nāṭṭup paḻiya makkaḷ taṅkaḷ ālavāy avircaṭik kaṭavuḷic cokkaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ.  (accokkaṉ piṉṉic cuntarēcaṉāṉāṉ)
cokkaṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkuriya poruḷeṉṉi?  muḻu aḻakaṉ - pēraḻakaṉ eṉpatu.  cokku - pēraḻaku.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭil ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkum maṟṟoru paḻampatiyākiya tirumāliruñ cōliyil eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa kaṭavuḷit tamiḻ makkaḷ aḻakaṉ eṉṟu pōṟṟi yiruttaliyum uṉṉuka.
aḻakaṉē ālavāyil appaṉē
	aruḷ ceyvāyē	- appar
takkaṉ vēḷvi takarttaruḷ ālavāy
	cokkaṉē yañca leṉṟaruḷ ceyyeṉi	 
		- ñāṉacampantar
mokkaṇi yataṉiṉ muḻuttaḻal mēṉi
	cokka tākak kāṭṭiya toṉmiyum	- māṇikka vācakar
palapalanāmam collip paḻittacicu pālaṉtaṉṉi
alavalimi tavirtta aḻakaṉ alaṅkāraṉmali
kulamali kōlamali kuḷirmāmali koṟṟamali
nilamali nīṇṭamali tirumāliruñ cōliyatē	
		- periyāḻvār
paṇṭit tamiḻar aḻaki allatu murukip poru
ḷākak koṇṭu vaḻipāṭu nikaḻtti vantaṉareṉpatu veḷ
ḷiṭimali.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ, maṇam, iḷami, kaṭavuṭ
ṭaṉmi, aḻaku ākiya ivikaḷiṉ poruḷāka murukaṉ poli
talum, innāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟāya aḻakiṭi ēṉiya mūṉṟum piri
viṉṟi virāvi niṟṟalum, ataṉāṉ avvirāvutaluṭiya aḻaki murukeṉak kōṭalum, piṟavum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
murukil ivviyalpukaḷ keḻumiyiruttali yuṇarnta pulavar pallōr.  avaruḷ paḻiyavar nakkīraṉār.  ivar aru
ḷiya tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyi, murukil oḷirum nuṇṇiyal
kaḷiṉ parumi allatu pariṇāmam eṉṟu kūṟalām.  nūl muṟ
ṟum avviyalpukaḷ potuḷi niṟpiṉum, avi veḷippaṭi
yākavum viḷakkamākavum nūliṉ iṟuvāyil āciriyarāl niralē peyyappeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.  kīraṉār, arum peṟaṉ marapiṉ perum peyar muruka eṉṟu murukiṉ peyar vaḻit
toṉmi kuṟittu, ammurukil tikaḻum aḻakiyum, avvaḻaki
ṉūṭē piriviṉṟi virāvi niṟkum maṇam, iḷami, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi eṉṉum ivikaḷiyum oru cēravittu, aṇaṅkucāl uyar
nili taḻīip paṇṭittaṉ - maṇaṅkamaḻ teyvat tiḷanalaṅ kāṭṭi eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.  aṇaṅkiṟku īṇṭu aḻakup poruḷ koḷka.

3. murukiṉ toṉmi
muruku eṉṉuñ col varalāṟu - muruku murukaṉāṉami - murukaṉ colvaḻakkuk kālam - murukaṉ toṉmikku iraṇṭu kuṟippukkaḷ - mutaṉ makaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭamum makkaḷ vāḻvu mutal mutal toṭaṅkappaṭṭa iṭamum - tamiḻariṉ malivāḻvum murukum - peyariṉ paṉmiyum poruḷiṉ orumiyum - nālvaki nilattil aḻakiṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷ - tolkāppiyak kālam - tolkāppiyattukku muṉṉarē murukaṉ vaḻipāṭuṇmi - vilaṅkum mutal makaṉum - hekkal, ṭārviṉ koḷki - uḷḷatu ciṟattal - tirumāl pattup
piṟavi nuṭpam - paḻiya makaṉ pallamippu - pallukkum mūḷikku
muḷḷa iyipu - makaṉukkuk kaṭavuḷuṇarvuṟṟa kālam - makkaḷ mākkaḷ nili - mutaṉ makkaḷiṉ uṇavu mutaliyaṉa - makkaḷ aṭṭil toḻil koṇṭa kālam - iyaṟki murukaṉ anāti.)
muruku eṉṉuñ colvaḻakku ennāḷil uṇṭāyiṟṟu?  ataṉ varalāṟeṉṉi?  muruku eṉpatu mikat toṉmi vāynta oru tamiḻc ceñcol.  paṇṭi nāḷil tamiḻc coṟ
kaḷ pala mutalniliyaḷavāka niṉṟu āṭcipeṟṟu vantaṉa.  piṉṉē nāḷaṭivil avikaḷuḷ pala iṭinili iṟuti
nilikaḷ peṟalāyiṉa.  accoṟkaḷuḷ muruku eṉpatum oṉṟu.   muruku eṉṉum mutalnili aṉ eṉṉum āṇpāl iṟuti niliyēṟṟu murukaṉ āyiṟṟu.  ākavē murukiṉiṉṟum murukaṉ piṟantāṉeṉka.  murukukkum murukaṉukkum poruḷil vēṟ
ṟumi yuṇṭōveṉil illi yeṉka.  iraṇṭum oru poruḷiyē kuṟippaṉa.  murukē murukaṉ;  murukaṉē muruku.
muruku allatu murukaṉ eṉṉuñ col vaḻakku mikamikat toṉmiyatu.  accol vaḻakku innāḷiyatu eṉṟu eva
rāṉum variyaṟuttuk kūṟal iyalātu.  toṉmi vāynta murukaṉ eṉṉun tamiḻccol iṉṉum iṟantupaṭavilli;  iṉṉum āṭci peṟṟē niṟkiṟatu.  accolvaḻi ataṟkuriya poruḷi iṉṉum pōṟṟuvōruḷar;  pāṭuvōruḷar.  iṉ
ṟum appeyarāṉ viḻākkaḷ naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa;  nōṉpukaḷ koṇṭāṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  accolliṉ uṟutiyum māṇpum tūymiyum eṉṉē eṉṉē eṉṟu iṟumpūteytukiṟēṉ.
murukiṉ toṉmi kāṇac ciṟappāka īṇṭu iraṇṭu kuṟippukkaḷ poṟikkalām.  oṉṟu murukaṉ tamiḻk kaṭavuḷ eṉpatu;  maṟṟoṉṟu murukaṉ mali nilak kaṭavuḷ eṉpatu.  ivviraṇṭāṉum murukaṉ toṉmi naṉi viḷaṅkum.  tami
ḻaṉ, tamiḻp perumāṉ, tamiḻ iṟi, muttamiḻōṉ eṉavum, kuṟiñci kiḻāṉ, mali kiḻavōṉ eṉavun tamiḻp periyōr murukaṉi aḻittiruttal kāṇka.
tamiḻiṉ mutumiyiyum, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ paḻamiyiyum īṇṭu virikkiṟ perukum.  mutaṉ makaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭam tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṭa uṇmi yoṉṟi īṇṭu niṉivūṭṭuvatu cālum.  ippaḻam peru nāṭṭār kaṭa
vuḷukku mutal mutal tūviya coṉmalar muruku eṉil, ataṉ toṉmiyi eṉṉeṉṟu iyampuvatu!
makkaḷ vāḻvu mutal mutal toṭaṅkappaṭṭa iṭam mali eṉpatu aṟiñar koḷki.  iḵtu iyaṟki nūlāciriyar palar oppamuṭinta uṇmi.  maliyil vāḻnta paḻiya makkaḷ taṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷik kuṟiñci kiḻāṉ malikiḻavōṉ eṉṟu kōṭal iyalpu.  paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ maliyiṭi vāḻntapōtu, taṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷi malinilak kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭatil viyappeṉṉi?  makkaḷiṉ nalvāḻvu maliyiṭi arumpiya pōḻtu murukeṉuñ collum alarntatu.  ittakiya colliṉ toṉmiyi eṅṅaṉam aḷanturippatu?
maliyil vāḻnta tamiḻ makkaḷ, piṉṉip piṟa nilaṅ
kaḷil kuṭipukuntu, avvan nilaṅkaḷiṉ iyalpukkēṟpa muḻu
mutalukkup palavēṟu peyariṭṭārkaḷ.  palavēṟu peyarkaḷik
koṇṭu, kaṭavuḷarum palar eṉṟu kōṭalākātu.  nilattiṉ iyalpiṟkēṟpa iṭappaṭṭa peyarkaḷ palavāyiṉum, avi yāvum oru poruḷiyē kuṟikkoṇṭu niṟpaṉavām.  av
voru poruḷ etu?  aḵtu iyaṟkiyiluḷḷa aḻaku allatu muruku eṉka.  iyaṟki yaḻaku kāṭākap pacumik kāṭci vaḻaṅkiya
pōtu, avvaḻaki allatu murukit tamiḻmakkaḷ tirumāl eṉṟārkaḷ.  1maṟṟa nilakkaṭavuḷ peyarkaḷiyum ivvāṟē koḷka.  tamiḻ makkaḷ aḻakeṉum murukip poruḷākak koṇṭatoṉṟē īṇṭuk karutaṟpāṟṟu.  tamiḻ makkaḷ vāḻvu, eṉṟu malinilattil tuvaṅkappaṭṭatō, aṉṟu
toṭṭu iṉṟuvari, murukaṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭut teyvamākap pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṟāṉ.  tamiḻnāṭṭup paḻaṅkaṭavuḷ murukaṉē.
tamiḻ nāṭṭippaṟṟiya evvārāyccikkun tolkāppi
yattik karuviyākak koḷvatu vaḻakkam.
eṉṉi?  ippoḻutuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷuḷ tol
kāppiyam paḻamiyuṭiyatākaliṉ eṉka.  tolkāppiyak kāla ārāycci iṉṉum muṟṟuppeṟavilli.  aḵtu iṉṉum vaḷarntu celkiṟatu. atu kuṟittu itukāṟum ārāyntavark
kuḷḷuṅ karuttu vēṟṟumi ulavi varukiṟatu.  tolkāppiyak kālam ittuṇi āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar muṉṉar eṉṟu -ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṟārēyaṉṟi ēṟakkuṟiya ātal ikkālam eṉṟu evarum aṟutiyiṭṭuk kūṟiṉārilli.  tolkāppiyaṉār kālam mūvāyiram, aiyāyiram, patiṉāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar iruttalvēṇṭumeṉṟu muṟi muṟiyē  cilar kiḷantu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  tolkāppiyaṉār kālam ettuṇi āyiram āyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar āyiṉumāka.   avar kālattil murukaṉ vaḻipāṭu iruntatā illiyā eṉpatoṉṟē īṇṭu ārāyaṟpālatu.
tolkāppiyaṉār kālattil murukaṉ vaḻipāṭu irun
tatu eṉpataṟku avartam nūliṉuḷḷē akaccāṉṟukaḷuṇṭu.  akkālattil murukaṉ vaḻipāṭu iruntatu uṇmi.  ap
poḻutu aḵtu enniliyiliruntatu?  toṭakka niliyilā allatu paḻaki vaḷam paṭṭa niliyilā?  paḻakip paḻaki mutirntu vaḷampaṭṭa niliyil avvaḻipāṭu annāḷil iruntatu.  ākavē tolkāppiyaṉār kālattukku muṉṉarē tamiḻ nāṭṭil murukaṉ vaḻipāṭu āṭciyiliruntateṉak koḷka.  eppaṭi?
tolkāppiyattil nālvaki nilaṉkaḷum, avvannila iyaluk kēṟṟavāṟu makkaḷ koṇṭa vaḻakka oḻukkaṅkaḷum, piṟa vāḻvut tuṟikaḷum pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  appēccāṉ tolkāppiyak kālattukku muṉṉarē tamiḻ makkaḷ kuṟiñci nilam viṭuttu, maṟṟa mulli marutam neytal nilaṅkaḷil kuṭipukuntu, vāḻvu naṭāttiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  tamiḻ makkaḷ nālvaki nilaṅkaḷilum vāḻvu tuvaṅkā muṉṉar atāvatu avarkaḷ malinilattil maṭṭum vāḻnta vēḷi
yil, avarkaḷ murukaṉ vaḻipāṭṭit toṭaṅkip payiṟci peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.  itu murukaṉukkuriya kuṟiñci kiḻāṉ, mali kiḻavōṉ mutaliya ciṟappup peyarkaḷāṉ aṟiyak kiṭakkiṟatu.  maliyiṭimaṭṭum makkaḷ vāḻvu tuvaṅkiya nāḷ ennāḷō?
maliyiṭi makkaḷ nalvāḻvu celuttap pukumuṉṉar makkaḷ eppaṭi iruntārkaḷ?  makkaḷ tōṟṟa varalāṟṟi ārāyntāl palatiṟa uṇmikaḷ pōtarum.  makkaḷ tōṟṟa varalāṟṟip paṟṟic camaya nūlkaḷ palavāṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa.  avi nampikki yulakukku uriyaṉavām.  iyaṟkit tuṇikoṇṭu ārāyntu muṭivu kaṇṭa aṟiñar, makkaḷiṉ mūtātikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷeṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.  makkaḷulakiṉ tōṟṟuvāykku nilikkaḷaṉ vilaṅku ulakam eṉpatu ak kūrttamatiyiṉar ārāycciyiṟ pōnta uṇmi.  ivvārāycciyiṟ pukuntu paṉṉeṭu nāḷ uḻittup palatiṟa nuṭpaṅkaḷi ulakiṟkuṇart
tiya hekkal, ṭārviṉ mutaliya pēraṟiñarkku ulakaṅ kaṭamip
paṭuvatāka.
namatu nāṭṭup purāṇaṅkaḷ cila ivvuṇmiyi oru
vāṟu katikaḷ vaṭivāka aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.  purāṇa nūl
kaḷil pala iṭaṅkaḷil tirumāliṉ pattup piṟavikaḷ collap
paṭukiṉṟaṉa.  kūrtal allatu uḷḷatu ciṟattal (Evolution) muṟipaṟṟi ap pattiyum uṟṟu nōkkiṉ,  paṭippaṭiyāka uyirkaḷiṉ piṟavi vaḷarcci pulaṉākum.  nam nāṭṭup purāṇi
karkaḷ periya hekkalkaḷ, ṭārviṉkaḷ pōlum!
vilaṅkiṉiṉṟum piṟanta makaṉukkut toṭakkattil pala
tiṟa vilaṅkuk kūṟukaḷiruntaṉa.  avikaḷuḷ oṉṟu pal.  annāḷiya makaṉukkuk kūriya vāḷaṉiya kōrip paṟka
ḷiruntaṉa.  ap pal nāḷaṭivil tēyntu tēyntu ippoḻu
tuḷḷa niliyi aṭintirukkiṟatu.  kōrip palliṉiṉṟun tēyvuṟṟa appallukku iṉṉum nāyppal eṉṉum vaḻak
kiruttali yōrka.  kōrip palleṉum vilaṅkup pal
luṭiya pāvikaḷ iṉṉuṅ kōyilkaḷil niṉṟukoṇ
ṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  avi namatu mūtātikaḷiṉ niṉivuk kuṟi
kaḷākum.
vilaṅkiṉiṉṟum piṟanta makaṉukkuk kaṭavuḷ uṇarveṉum aṉpuneṟi uṭaṉē viḷaṅkiyirātu.  pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉarē avaṉukkuk kaṭavuḷ uṇarvu tōṉṟiyirukkum.  eppōtu avvuṇarvu tōṉṟiyirukkalām?
nīṇṭa kōrippal vilaṅkuṇarviṟku aṟikuṟi, aḵtiruntamaṭṭum makaṉukkuk kaṭavuḷ uṇarvu tōṉṟiyirātu.  pallukkum, kuṭariṉ uḷḷuṟuppuc cilavaṟṟiṟkum, mūḷikkun toṭarpuṇṭu.  pal tēyum aḷaviṉatāka uḷḷuṟuppukkaḷun tēyum.  avvuṟuppukkaḷ tēyum aḷaviṉatāka mūḷiyiṉ vaṉmi aruki meṉmi perukum.  meṉmi aḷavāka aṟivu viḷakkamuṟum.  cila āyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar irunta makkaḷiṉ uḷḷuṟuppukkaṭkum, cila nūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar irunta makkaḷiṉ uḷḷuṟuppukkaṭkum, ippōtiya makkaḷiṉ avvuṟuppukkaṭkum vēṟṟumiyuṇṭu.  ippoḻutuñ cila uḷḷuṟuppukkaḷ payaṉiṉṟik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu maruttuva aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.  ivvirinta ārāycciyil īṇṭu nuḻiya vēṇṭuvatilli.  vilaṅkiṉiṉṟum piṟanta makaṉ eppoḻutu kaṭavuḷ uṇarvu peṟṟiruppāṉ eṉpatoṉṟē īṇṭu namakkut tēvi.
vilaṅkiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiya makaṉ, vaṭivāṉ vilaṅkoṭu vēṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkiṉum, uṇarvāl nīṇṭanāḷ ataṉoṭu  vēṟupaṭāmaliruntāṉ.  piṉṉip pal mutaliyaṉa  tēyvuṟa vuṟa uṇarvāṉum avaṉ vilaṅkiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭalāṉāṉ.  āki
yum makaṉ iṉṉum vilaṅkuṇarviṉiṉṟum muṟṟum viṭutali
yaṭintāṉilli.  viṭutaliyaṭiyāmiyāṉ avaṉ iṉat
tuḷ iru pirivu tōṉṟalāyiṉa.  oṉṟu mākkaḷ iṉam;  maṟ
ṟoṉṟu makkaḷ iṉam.  mākkaḷāvār vilaṅki yotta aiyaṟi
vuṭiyār;  makkaḷāvār āṟaṟivuṭiyār.  māvu mākkaḷum aiyaṟi viṉavē makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē eṉṟār tol
kāppiyaṉār.  ammākkaḷum makkaḷuñ cērnta oṉṟē maṉpati eṉpatu.
āṟāvatu aṟivu etu?  avvaṟivē kaṭavuḷ uṇarveṉum aṉpuneṟi viḷakkattukku nilikkaḷaṉā yiruppatu.  avvaṟivu viḷaṅkiya makkaḷ, avvaṟivu viḷaṅkāta mākkaḷiṉiṉṟum pirivuṟṟapōḻtu, avarkaḷiṭi (makkaḷiṭi)k kaṭavuḷ uṇarvu arumpiyiruttal vēṇṭum.  akkaṭavuḷ uṇarvē makkaḷiyum mākkaḷiyum vēṟupaṭuttuvateṉka.  makkaḷiṭik kaṭavuḷuṇarvu, avarkaḷ maliyiṭimaṭṭum vāḻnta pōtu tōṉṟiṉamiyāl, avarkaḷ kaṭavuḷik kuṟiñci kiḻāṉ, malikiḻavōṉ eṉṟu pōṟṟiṉārkaḷ pōlum!
mākkaḷ makkaḷ iyalpukaḷum tolkāppiyaṉār kālat
til mutirnta āṭciyiliruntaṉa.  ātalāl avvēṟṟumi tōṉṟiya kālamum tolkāppiyattukku muntiyatē.  av
vēṟṟumiyuṟṟa kālattik kaṇittuk kūṟal eḷitō?  āta
lāl murukaṉ toṉmiyik kālavariyaṟippaṭuttik kūṟalum eḷitākuṅkol?
makaṉ toṭakkattil kāykaṟikaḷi avittut tiṉṟā
ṉilli.  avaṉ avaṟṟi iyaṟkiyākavē uṇṭu kaḻittu van
tāṉ.  piṉṉi avaṉ pallāyiram āṇṭu kaṭantu kāykaṟi
kaḷi avittut tiṉṉalāṉāṉ.  akkālatti iyaṟki nūl vallār cilar iṟṟikkuc cumār muppatāyiram āṇṭiṟku muṉṉateṉac colkiṟār.
mutal mutal maliyiṭi vāḻvu tuvakkiya makkaḷ, kāykaṟikaḷip pacumiyākavē uṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.  avarkaḷ murukaṉukkut tēṉum tiṉiyum vittu vaḻipaṭṭati aṉparkaṭku niṉivūṭṭukiṟēṉ.  maṉpatikkuḷ mākkaḷ makkaḷ vēṟṟumi pulaṉāyk kaṭavuḷ uṇarvu peṟṟapōtum, makkaḷ kāykaṟikaḷip pacumiyākavē pucittiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu ūkittuṇaralām.  annāḷ muppatiṉāyiram āṇṭiṟku mēm
paṭṭu, ettuṇi āyiram āṇṭu kaṭantu niṟkumō teriya
villi.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟivanta cila kuṟippukkaḷāṉ, muruku eṉ
ṉuñ col vaḻakku ennāḷil uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kāṇṭal iyalāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu.  piṉṉi emmuṭipu koḷvatu?  maṉ
patikkuḷ mākkaḷ makkaḷ vēṟṟumiyuṇarvu tōṉṟi, makkaḷiṉattil kaṭavuḷ aṟivu arumpiya nāḷtoṭṭu, muruku eṉṉuñ  colvaḻakakut tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.  an
nāḷ iṉṉum ārāycci yulakiṟku eṭṭavilli.
murukeṉuñ colvaḻakkuk kālattik kāṇṭalē aritāyirukkiṟatu.  aṅṅaṉamāka, accoṟporuḷ kālatti ārāyap pukuvatu aṟiyāmiyākavē muṭiyum.  ivvārāycci
yil talippaṭa vēṇṭuvatilli.  murukeṉuñ cemporuḷ kāla ellikku uṭpaṭṭataṉṟu eṉṟu koḷvatē aṟivuṭimi.  murukaṉ kālaṅ kaṭanta kaṭavuḷ.  iyaṟki eṉṟu uṇṭō, aṉṟu mutal murukaṉum uḷaṉ.  iyaṟki anāti;  murukaṉum anāti.
maliyiṭi vāḻnta  tamiḻ makkaḷ taṅkaṭkuk kaṇkūṭākak kāṭciyaḷitta iyaṟkiyi aṭikkaṭi kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭu, uḷat
tāl mukantu, ati iṭiyaṟātu niṉintu niṉintu, ataṉ
kaṇ paṭinta vāḻvu naṭāttiṉārkaḷ.  ataṉ payaṉāka avarkaḷ iyaṟkiyiṉuḷ piriviṉṟi virāvi niṟkum aḻakiyum, aḻakiṉ māṟāta iḷamiyiyum, iḷamiyilūrum aḻiyā maṇatti
yum, iviyuṭiya oṉṟu ellāvaṟṟiyuṅ kaṭantu māṟutaliṉṟi yoḷirun taṉmiyiyuṅ kaṇṭārkaḷ.  ivvaḻaku, iḷami, maṇam, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi ākiya iyalpukaḷik koṇṭa oṉṟip paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ muruku eṉṉuñ collāl aḻittup pōṟṟiṉārkaḷ.  muruku eṉpatu poruḷ potinta oru col.  ammurukiyuṭiyavaṉ murukaṉ.

4. iyaṟkivaḻi aḻakik kāṇṭal
(murukum iyaṟkiyum uyirkaḷum - uyirkaṭku murukaṉ iyaṟki vāyilākap purintuvarun tuṇi - uyirkaḷ murukaṉātal - ataṟkuriya vaḻi - eṇṇam - akam puṟam - iyaṟki vaḻipāṭu - pula
ṉaṭakkattiṉ nuṭpam - tirumūlar - āṇṭakimi - apparum 
pulaṉaṭakkamum - iṟiyiyalpu - iyaṟkikkum pulaṉkaṭkumuḷḷa toṭarpu - iyaṟkiyum pulaṉkaḷum - pulavaṉ iyaṟkiyiṉ 
makaṉ - iyaṟki vaḻipāṭṭiṉ kūṟupāṭu - aḻakup poruḷkaḷ - peṇmi vaḻipāṭu.)
murukaṉ eṉṟum iḷiyaṉāy, maṇamuṭiyaṉāyk, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi yuṭiyaṉāy, aḻakuṭiyavaṉāyiruttalāl uyirkaṭ keṉṉi?  uyirkaḷ avviyalkaḷip peṟum vaḻi yuṇṭukol?
murukaṉpāl avviyalpukaḷ iyaṟkiyāy amintu kiṭattaṟkup poruḷuṇṭu.  uyirkaḷum annili eytal vēṇṭum eṉpatu murukaṉ tiruvuḷḷakkiṭakki.  iyaṟkikkum murukaṉukkumuḷḷa toṭarpippōl murukaṉukkum uyir
kaṭkun toṭarpuṇṭu.  ittoṭarpi uṇaravēṇṭuvatu uyirkaḷiṉ kaṭami.  murukaṉō uyirkaḷit taṉṉip pōlākka āval koṇṭu niṟkiṟāṉ.  avvāvalaṉṟi vēṟu āval avaṉukkilli.  avvāvaluṭaṉ eppoḻutum murukaṉ iyaṟki vāyilāka uyirkaṭkut tuṇi purintum varukiṟāṉ.  ñāyiṟu, kāṟṟu, puṉal mutaliya iyaṟkip poruṭkaḷiṉ vāyilāka, murukaṉ uyirkaḷi ōmpā toḻivaṉēl, ulaka
mētu?  uyirētu?  vāḻvētu?
nāṭōṟum iyaṟki vāyilākat tāṅkaḷ peṟṟu varū
um tuṇi iyaṟkiyiṉuṭiyatā allatu ataṉ uḷḷuṟi
yāka uḷḷa murukaṉuṭiyatā eṉpati uyirkaḷ uyttuṇaral vēṇṭum.  attuṇi, iyaṟkiyiṉuṭiyatu eṉṟu karutu
vōr, iḷami maṇam kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi aḻaku ākiyavaṟṟip peṟal aritu.  iyaṟki vāyilāka murukaṉuṭimiyām at
tuṇi peṟuvati uṇarvōr murukaṉākalām;  atāvatu avaṉatu iyalpukaḷip peṟalām.
murukaṉ iyalpukaḷāya iyaṟki maṇamum, māṟā iḷami
yum, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyum, aḻiyā aḻakum peṟa ovvoruvarum virumpuvar.  avaṟṟi virumpātār ariyar. veṟum viruppa
maṭṭuṅ koḷvatiṟ payaṉilli.  viruppatti niṟivēṟṟik
koḷḷalē aṟivuṭimi.  ati evvāṟu niṟivēṟṟik koḷvatu?  emmuṟi paṟṟi niṟivēṟṟik koḷvatu?
mutalāvatu vēṇṭaṟpālatu murukip paṟṟiya eṇṇam.  murukaṉi eṇṇumpōtellām avaṉ maṇamuṭiyāṉ, iḷamiyuṭiyāṉ, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyuṭiyāṉ, aḻakuṭi
yāṉ eṉṟu niṉittal vēṇṭum.  anniṉivu kūṭutaṟkup puṟattē avviyalpukaḷuṭiya iyaṟkip poruṭkaṇmītu karuttip patiya vittalvēṇṭum.  akattil oṉṟu nili
peṟutaṟku ataṉ toṭarpāyuḷḷa puṟamum ataṉuṭaṉ oṉṟu
tal vēṇṭum.  eṉṉi?  akamē puṟamākaliṉ eṉka.  iraṇ
ṭiṟkun toṭarpilā vāḻvu cemmiyatākātu.  ātalāl, muruki uṇartaṟku akappuṟa oṟṟumi iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
akattiṉ vāyilākap puṟattiṟ pukutal orumuṟi.  puṟattiṉ vāyilāka akatti aṇivatu iṉṉoru muṟi.  iraṇṭa
ṉuḷ muṉṉiyatu mika ariyatu;  iṭarppāṭuṭiyatu;  muṭivu kāṇṭal eṉṉum uṟutiyuṅ kūṭṭātatu.  piṉṉiyatō eḷiyatu;  iyaṟkiyil iyaṅkuvatu;  uṟuti kūṭṭuvatu.  ākavē puṟavaḻi paṟṟutaliyē īṇṭu yāṉ koḷkiṟēṉ.
murukaṉ kuṇaṅkuṟi kaṭantavaṉ.  avaṉatu iyalpukaḷi uṇarvil- nikaḻcciyil peṟutalē iyalum.  uṇarvil - nikaḻcciyil avikaḷip peṟutaṟkum toṭakkattil avi
kaḷiṉ parumi niṉivu vēṇṭaṟpālatu.  itaṟku vaḻi eṉṉi?
murukaṉ kuṇaṅkuṟi kaṭanta oruvaṉāyiṉum, iyaṟki avaṉukku uṭalāka amintirukkiṟatu.  avvuṭal vaḻi uyirām muruki yuṇartal kūṭum.  ākavē muruki uṇara virumpuvōr iyaṟkiyip paṟṟuk kōṭākak kōṭal vēṇṭum.  koḷ
ḷiṉ, muruki iyaṟki uṇarttum.  uyirkaḷ iyaṟkiyōṭu uṟavu koḷḷum aḷaviṉatāka, avikaḷiṉ māṭṭu murukaṉ uṇarvum perukkeṭukkum   iḵteṉṉa!  iyaṟki vaṭivaṅ
kaḷi nāṭōṟum kāṇkiṟōm;  iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ vāḻkiṟōm.  murukik kāṇkiṟōmilliyē eṉṟu cilar karutalām.  kāṇpaṉavellām kāṭciyākā.  aṅṅaṉē vāḻvellām vāḻvākā.  pulaṉkaḷit tīya vaḻiyil celuttikkoṇṭu, iyaṟkiyi orōvaḻik kāṇṭalum, ataṉuṭaṉ vāḻalum muṟiyē atik kāṇpatumākātu;  ataṉōṭu vāḻvatu
mākātu.  muruki iyaṟki uṇarttum eṉṉum uṟuti
yōṭu, pulaṉkaḷi iyaṟkikku māṟupaṭṭa neṟikaḷil tōya
viṭātu, avikaḷi iyaṟkiyil nilipeṟutti, iyaṟki
yuṭaṉ kalanta uṟavu koḷḷap payilalvēṇṭum.  ippayiṟcikku mutal mutal vēṇṭaṟpālatu pulaṉ tūymi.
pulaṉkaḷiṉ payiṟcikkēṟṟa vaṇṇam ulakil vāḻvu amikiṟatu.  pulaṉkaḷ tīyavaḻiyil uḻaliṉ, vāḻvun tīyatākum.  avi nalla vaḻiyil iyaṅkiṉ, vāḻvum nallatākum.  īṇṭut tīyavaḻi nallavaḻi eṉpaṉa muṟiyē ceyaṟki iyaṟki vaḻi
kaḷik kuṟippaṉavām.  pulaṉkaḷ niliyi vāḻvu poṟuttu niṟṟalāṉ. appulaṉkaḷit tūymippaṭuttuvatil, iyaṟki
vaḻi murukik kāṇa viḻivōr kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyirup
pārāka.
iṟiyi uṇartaṟkup pulaṉkaḷi aṭakkumāṟu āṉ
ṟōr aṟivuṟuttiyiruppa, nīvir appulaṉkaḷit tūymip
paṭuttuṅkaḷ eṉṟu navilkiṟīr.  pulaṉkaḷi aṭakkātu āṭa
viṭutalāl nalaṉ viḷiyuṅkol eṉṟu cilar viṉavalām.  ciṉṉāḷākap pulaṉaṭakkam eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar taṉakkuriya poruḷiḻantu niṟkiṟatu.  pulaṉaṭakkam eṉpataṟkup pulaṉi oṭukki aḻittal eṉṉum poruḷ collappaṭukiṟatu.  atu tavaṟu.  pulaṉ keṭṭāl aṟivētu?  vāḻvētu?
yōkam yōkam eṉṟu cillōr pulaṉkaḷi oṭukki oṭukki muṭivil avikaḷik keṭuttuk kuruṭarāyc, ceviṭa
rāy, nōyarāy māḷkiṟār.  pulaṉkeṭṭa oṉṟu aṟivaṟṟa poruḷātal veḷḷiṭimali.  aṟiviṉ ākkattukkeṉap paṭikkappaṭṭa pulaṉkaḷi aṟivuk kēṭṭiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttal ettakiya maṭami?  pulaṉkaḷ oṭukkappaṭuvaṉavāyiṉ, avi ēṉ paṭippil amital vēṇṭum?  āṇṭavaṉ paṭippil poruḷaṟṟatoṉṟu tōṉṟumā,  tōṉṟavē tōṉṟātu.  āṇṭavaṉ paṭippil  tōṉṟiyuḷḷa ovvoṉṟum poru
ḷuṭiyatu;  arumiyāṉatu;  vāḻviṟkuriyatu.
pulaṉkaḷ makkaḷ vāḻviṟkeṉap paṭippil amin
tuḷḷa aṟivuk karuvikaḷ.  avikaḷiṉ vāyilākavē uyirkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum uḷattāl uṇartal vēṇṭum.  pulaṉkaḷ puṟattiyum akattiyum āṟṟuppaṭuttum perumpulavarkaḷ.  attakip pulavarkaḷi aṭakkuvatum oṭukkuvatum iyaṟ
kikku māṟupaṭṭu naṭattalākum.
pulaṉaṭaṅki vāḻumāṟu āṉṟōr aruḷiyataṉ karut
teṉṉi?  pulaṉaṭaṅki vāḻumāṟu āṉṟōr aruḷiya mey
yurikaḷip poṉṉēpōl pōṟṟukiṟēṉ.  pulaṉaṭakkam eṉpataṟku āṉṟōr koṇṭa karutti uṇartalvēṇṭum.  aṉṉār pulaṉkaḷik keṭuttoḻikkumāṟu orupōtum aṟivuṟuttiṉārilli.  pulaṉaṭakkattip piṟaḻakkoṇṭu, pulaṉkaḷik keṭuttu, ulakam iṭarppaṭap pōkiṟateṉṟu añci, yōka neṟiyil tērccipeṟṟa tirumūlaṉār, 
añcum aṭakkaṭak keṉpar aṟivilār
añcum aṭakkum amararum aṅkili
añcum aṭakkil acētaṉam ākumeṉṟiṭṭu
añcum aṭakkā aṟivaṟin tēṉē
eṉṟu kūṟip pōntār.  ittirumūlaṉārē pala iṭaṅkaḷiṟ pula
ṉaṭakkaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār.  appulaṉ aṭakkam eṉpataṟkup pulaṉ kēṭu eṉpatu poruḷāyiṉ, avar añcum aṭakkaṭak keṉpar aṟivilār eṉṉun tirumantiratti ōtiyirār.  ākavē, pulaṉaṭakkam eṉpataṉ poruḷi ōrtal vēṇṭum.
pulaṉaṭakka meṉṟāl eṉṉi?  pulaṉkaḷit tīya vaḻiyil cellātavāṟu kāttalē pulaṉaṭakka meṉpatu;  pulaṉkaḷik keṭuttal eṉpataṉṟu.  nāvaṭakkam - vāyaṭakkam - kiyaṭakkam eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.  oṉṟiyun tiṉṉātē;  pēcātē;  ceyyātē eṉṟu muṟiyē poruḷ koḷḷalām pōlum!  poruntiya uṇavi aḷavāka uṇ;  tīyaṉavaṟṟiyum payaṉil coṟkaḷiyum pēcātu vāymiyip pēcu;  piṟar poruḷ kavaral mutaliyaṉavaṟṟic ceyyātu nallaṉavaṟṟic cey eṉṟu avvaḻakkut toṭarkaṭkup poruḷ kūṟal marapu.  aṅṅaṉē pulaṉ aṭakkam eṉpataṟkum poruḷ kōṭal vēṇṭum.  pulaṉaṭakkam eṉpatu pulaṉ aḻivik kuṟippataṉṟu.  ittoṭar pulaṉkaḷi neṟiyallā neṟiyil celuttāmiyik kuṟippateṉka.
iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ uṟavukoṇṭu, puṟattiyum akattiyum oṉṟac ceytu, uyirkaṭku iṉpamūṭṭap paṭippil amin
tuḷḷa pulaṉkaḷi avā eṉṉum pēykkum, maṟṟum pala ceyaṟki narakattuṟikaṭkum aṭimippaṭuttal iyaṟkiyi
yum, ataṉ uḷḷuṟiyām murukiyum maṟappatākum.  im
maṟappuḷḷa maṭṭun tuṉpam nīṅkātu.
ulakil āṇṭakimi eṉṉuñ col vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu.  etu āṇṭakimi?  avā mutaliya pēykaṭkup pulaṉkaḷi aṭimip
paṭuttātu kāppatē āṇṭakimi.  ivvāṇṭakimiyuṭiya oruvaṉē makaṉ;  oruttiyē makaḷ eṉka.
uraṉeṉṉun tōṭṭiyāṉ orintuṅ kāppāṉ
varaṉeṉṉum vippiṟkōr vittu
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār.
periyōr pulaṉkaḷi nōkki, nīṅkaḷ kaḷḷarkaḷ;  vañcakarkaḷ;  pēykaḷ;  uṅkaḷik kollalvēṇṭum;  aṭak
kalvēṇṭum;  oṭukkal vēṇṭum  eṉṟu palatiṟamākap pēci
yiruttalip paṟṟiyuñ cilarkku aiyan tōṉṟalām.  pulaṉkaḷ, avā mutaliya pēykaṭku eḷiyaṉavākumpōtu, avikaḷik kaṭintu, naṉṉeṟiyil tiruppavēṇṭi, avikaḷi nontum, vitum, kuṟi kūṟiyum periyōr pēcuvatu vaḻakkam.  itu kuṟittu īṇṭu virittuk kūṟal vēṇṭuvatilli.  itippaṟṟip peṇṇiṉ perumi allatu vāḻkkit tuṇi eṉṉum nūliṉum viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ.  īṅku oru periyār karutti eṭuttāḷum aḷavil niṉṟu mēṟcella virumpukiṟēṉ.  epperiyāri īṇṭuk koḷvatu nalaṉ?  akaviyilum, aṟivilum, aṉpilum mutirnta apparemperumāṉārik koḷkiṟēṉ.  apperiyār,
paṭukuḻip pavvat taṉṉa paṇṭiyip peyta vāṟṟāṟ
keṭuvatim maṉitarvāḻkki kāṇṭoṟuṅ kētukiṉṟēṉ
muṭukuva riruntu ḷivar mūrkkarē yivarka ḷōṭum
aṭiyaṉēṉ vāḻa māṭṭēṉ ārūrmū laṭṭa ṉīrē
puḻuppeyta paṇṭi taṉṉip puṟamoru tōlāṉ mūṭi
oḻukkaṟā voṉpatuvā yoṟṟumi yoṉṟu millic
caḻakkuṭi yitaṉu ḷivarcaṅkaṭam palavuñ ceyya
aḻippaṉāy vāḻa māṭṭēṉ ārūrmū laṭṭa ṉīrē
uyirnili yuṭampē kālā yuḷḷamē tāḻi yākat
tuyaramē yēṟṟa mākat tuṉpakkō lataṉip paṟṟip
payirtaṉik cuḻiya viṭṭup pāḻkkunī riṟittu mikka
ayarviṉā livark kāṟṟē ṉārūrmū laṭṭa ṉīrē
pattaṉāy vāḻa māṭṭēṉ pāviyēṉ paravi vantu
cittattu ḷivar tīya ceyviṉi palavuñ ceyya
mattuṟu tayirē pōla maṟukumeṉ ṉuḷḷan tāṉum
attaṉē amarar kōvē ārūrmū laṭṭa ṉīrē
puḷḷuva rivar kaḷvar  puṉattiṭip pukuntu niṉṟu
tuḷḷuvar cūṟi koḷvar tūneṟi viḷiya loṭṭār
muḷḷuṭi yavarkaḷ tammi mukkaṇāṉ pāta nīḻal
uḷḷiṭi maṟintu niṉṟaṅ kuṇarviṉā leyya lāmē
ittiruppākkaḷāl, pulaṉkaḷ tīyavaḻiyil tammi yīrttu alittaliyum, avikaḷiṉ koṭumikaḷiyum viḷakkiyavāṟum, akkoṭumikaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭutaliyaṭi
vāṉ āṇṭavaṉik kuṟiyirantu vēṇṭumāṟuṅ kāṇka.
tīya vaḻiyuḻalum pulaṉkaḷi aṭakkiyāḷalvēṇṭuvatu aṟiñar kaṭami.   appar cuvāmikaḷ pulaṉkaḷi nalvaḻippaṭut
tavē muyaṉṟār.  pulaṉkaḷit tīyavaḻiyiṉiṉṟuṅ kāttu, avikaḷi nalvaḻippaṭutta appar muyaṉṟārēyaṉṟi, avi
kaḷi aḻittut tāmum avikaḷuṭaṉ aḻiya muyaṉṟā
rilli.  tīneṟiyil tammi īrkkum pulaṉkaḷik kaṭintu, avikaḷi nōkki, nam āṇṭaki eṉṉa kaṭṭaḷi yiṭukiṟār pāruṅkaḷ!
kaṇkāḷ kāṇmiṉkaḷō - kaṭal
	nañcuṇṭa kaṇṭaṉ taṉṉi
eṇṭōḷ vīciniṉ ṟāṭum pirāṉtaṉṉik
	kaṇkāḷ kāṇmiṉkaḷō
cevikāḷ kēṇmiṉkaḷō - civaṉ
	emmiṟi cempavaḷa
eripōl mēṉippi rāṉṟiṟa meppōtuñ
	cevikāḷ kēṇmiṉkaḷō
mūkkē nīmuralāy - mutu
	kāṭuṟi mukkaṇaṉi
vākkē nōkkiya maṅki maṇāḷaṉi
	mūkkē nīmuralāy
vāyē vāḻttukaṇṭāy - mata
	yāṉi yuripōrttup
pēyvāḻ kāṭṭakat tāṭumpi rāṉtaṉi
	vāyē vāḻttu kaṇṭāy
ākki yāṟpayaṉeṉ - araṉ
	kōyil valamvantu
pūkki yālaṭṭip pōṟṟiyeṉ ṉātiv 
	vākki yāṟpayaṉeṉ
pulaṉkaḷ etaṟkākap paṭikkappaṭṭaṉa?  aṉparkaḷē!  appar aruṇmoḻiyi uṟṟu nōkkuṅkaḷ;  uṉṉuṅkaḷ;  pulaṉkaḷiṉ kaṭaṉi uṇaruṅkaḷ.  nalaṉ tarum pulaṉkaḷiyā keṭuppatu?  aḻippatu?  antō!  koṭumi!  koṭumi!  ākavē pulaṉkaḷit tīya pēy vaḻiyil uḻalātavāṟu kāttu, iṟi vaḻiyil tiruppuvatē avikaḷit tūymip paṭuttuva
tākum
iṟivaṉ kāṇṭaṟkariyaṉ;  māṟṟa maṉaṅ kaṭantu niṟkum maṟiyōṉ.  avaṉi uṇartaṟku avaṉ uṭalākavuḷḷa iyaṟkiyip paṟṟal vēṇṭum?   iyaṟkiyi evikaḷāl paṟṟutal vēṇṭum? puṟattukkum akattukkum naṭuvaṇ niṟkum pulaṉkaḷāleṉka.
iyaṟkikkum  pulaṉkaṭkumuḷḷa toṭarpu peritum uṉṉaṟpālatu. iyaṟkiyum pulaṉkaḷum iyintu niṟkumāṟē paṭippum amintirukkiṟatu.  makkaḷ uṭalil pulaṉkaḷ ēṉ amital vēṇṭum eṉṟu ciṟitu niṉintu ārāyntāl, appaṭippu nōkkam pulaṉākum.  iyaṟkiyiṉuṭaṉ uṟavu
koḷḷa vēṇṭuvatu pulaṉkaḷiṉ kaṭaṉ.  iyaṟkiyō pulaṉ
kaḷi viṭṭup pirivatilli.  atu pulaṉkaḷi eṉṟuṅ kavarnta
vaṇṇamirukkiṟatu.  kavarccikkuriya poruḷ iyaṟkiyiṉmāṭ ṭoḷirkiṟatu.
apporuḷeṉṉi?  aḵtu, iyaṟki vēṟu tāṉ vēṟu eṉṟu pirittuk kūṟutaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟi, iraṇṭum oṉṟē eṉṟu collat takkavāṟu niṟkum aḻaku allatu murukākum.  avviyaṟki yaḻaku pulaṉkaṭkup parumiyākap poruḷākātu, nuṇṇiyatāka nilavuvatāyiṉum, atu pulaṉkaḷ mītu nōkkaṅ koḷḷāta nēramilli.  pulaṉkaḷ vaḻi uyirkaḷit taṉ vaḻippaṭutta atu niṉiyāta nēramilli.  aḻaku, pulaṉkaḷit taṉvayappaṭuttak kāttuk koṇṭirukkiṟatu.  aḻaku, pulaṉkaḷ vāyilāka uḷattiṟ pukuntu, paṭintu, uyiriyum uṭaliyum taṉ mayamākka virintu niṟkiṟatu.  iḵtu aḻakiṉ iyalpu.
 aḻakiṉ kavarccikku eḷimiyākum iyalpum pulaṉkaṭku uṇṭu.  iṭiyiṭiyē pēyiṉ tūṇṭutalukku iriyākum iyalpum avikaṭku uṇṭu.  itaṉāl pulaṉkaḷiṉ iyal
pukkut takivu nērkiṟatu.  takivu pulaṉkaḷiṉ ceyaṟki nīrmi
yaṉṟi iyaṟki nīrmi yaṉṟu.  ceyaṟkit taṭiyi makkaḷ aṟivāl vilakkiṉ, pulaṉkaḷ taṅkaḷ iyaṟkit taṉmippaṭi aḻakiṉ kavarccikku eḷiyaṉavākum.  ivvāṟu eḷimi
yātalē pulaṉkaḷiṉ poruntiya iyalpeṉka.  iyaṟkikkum pulaṉkaṭkumuḷḷa iyipu kāntattukkum irumpukkumuḷḷa iyipiyottatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.  iṅṅaṉam pulaṉkaṭkuriya iyalpu vaḻi avikaḷi iyakkalē avikaḷit tūymip paṭuttuvatākum.
otta oṉṟi otta oṉṟuṭaṉ poruttuvatu arumi
yaṉṟu.  itu mika eḷitu.  toṭakkattil ciṟu muyaṟci ceytal vēṇṭum.  piṉṉar muyaṟciyiṉṟi viṉi iyalpāka nikaḻntu varum.  pulaṉkaḷ, māṟi niṉṟu mayakkum ceyaṟkip payiṟciyil nīṇṭanāḷ paḻakiyamiyāṉ, avikaṭkuriya iyalpil avi
kaḷik koṇarntu niṟuttac ciṟu muyaṟci vēṇṭaṟpālatē.  itu potu muṟi.  muyaṟciyiṉṟiyum cilarkku annili kūṭaluṇṭu.
toṭakkattil ciṟitu valintu muyaṉṟu pulaṉkaḷi iyaṟkiyil poruttiviṭiṉ, apporuttattukkeṉa etirnōkki niṟkum iyaṟki aḻaku allatu muruku avi
kaḷit taṉvaḻippaṭutti naṉṉeṟiyil niṟuttum poṟuppi ēṟṟukkoḷkiṟatu.  ākavē toṭakkattil ciṟumuyaṟciyil talippaṭal vēṇṭuvatu uyirkaḷiṉ kaṭami.
āṇṭavaṉ eṉakkup pulaṉkaḷi vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṟāṉ.  avaṉ pulaṉkaḷi eṉakku vaḻaṅkā tiruppiṉ piṟaviyāl yāṉ eṉṉa payaṉ peṟuvēṉ?  vāḻvi iyaṟki mayamākki, aḻa
kil mūḻki, iṉpa nukartaṟporuṭṭē pulaṉkaḷ āṇṭava
ṉāl eṉakku vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.  appulaṉkaḷik koṇṭu iyaṟkiyikkaṇṭavaṇṇamāy, ataṉ iciyik kēṭṭa vaṇṇamāy, ataṉ iṉimiyi nukarnta vaṇṇamāy, ataṉ maṇatti mukarnta vaṇṇamāy, ataṉ ūṟali ēṟṟa vaṇṇa
māy ulakil vāḻvu naṭāttap payila aṭiyēṉ perituṅ kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṟēṉ.  illiyēl yāṉ iṉpamiḻantu tuṉpakkuḻiyil vīḻntavaṉāvēṉ.
iyaṟki vāyilāka iṟivaṉik kāṇṭal vēṇṭum eṉṉum eṇṇam mutal mutal eṉatu neñcil uṟintu niṟṟal vēṇṭum.  avveṇṇam mutirntu mutirntu  pulaṉkaḷi iyaṟkiyil kaṭavukiṟatu.  pulaṉkaḷ, iyaṟkiyil paṭintu paṭintu, avviyaṟkiyiṉ kaṇ poliyum vaṭivilā aḻaki uḷḷattukku uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.  avvaḻaku pulaṉkaṭkum uḷḷattukkum viruntāki, akappuṟa vēṟṟumiyik kaḷintu ūṭṭum iṉpam colaṟpālataṉṟu.  nikaḻcciyil atu nukarattakkatu.
iyaṟkiyi eṇṇi eṇṇip pulaṉkaḷi iyaṟki
vaḻic celutti iṉpanukarkiṟavaṉē pulavaṉ.  appulavaṉuṭiya pulaṉkaḷum puntiyum iyaṟkiyil tōyntu oṉṟupaṭalāṉum, avvoṟṟumi akappuṟa vēṟṟumi uṇarvik kaḷita
lāṉum, avaṉ iyaṟki aṉṉiyiṉ makaṉākiṟāṉ.  iyaṟki
yiluḷḷa aḻakeṉum appaṉ avaṉit taṉvaṇṇamākku
kiṟāṉ.  ammakaṉip piṇi mūppu cākkāṭu aṇukā.  avaṉ aḻiyā aḻakaṉākiṟāṉ.  innili peṟutaṟkuk kaṭip
piṭikkum iyaṟki vāḻvē murukaṉaṭiyip pukalākak koḷva
teṉ ṟuṇarka.
iyaṟki vaḻipāṭṭiṟku eṅṅaṉam aṭikōlvatu?  aḻakō iyaṟkiyil yāṇṭum polikiṟatu.  iyaṟkiyil aḻakilā iṭamilli.  ivvaḻakorumi uṇarum perunili kūṭappeṟṟārkkup potuvāka iyaṟki vaḻipāṭeṉak kuṟittuviṭalām.  aḻakip paṉmiyāka uṇarum toṭakka niliyiliruppōrkku iyaṟki vaḻipāṭu eṉṟu potuvākak kuṟippatāl payaṉ viḷiyātu.  aṉṉār iyaṟki vaḻi
pāṭṭip paṭippaṭiyē toṭaṅkuvatu nalaṉ;  avar tamatu uḷḷattik kavarattakka iyaṟki aḻakup poruḷ etuvō, ataṉkaṇ avar pulaṉkaḷi oṉṟac ceyvārāka.  evaṉukku etaṉkaṇ pēraḻakiruppatākat tōṉṟukiṟatō, avaṉ ati iṭiyaṟātu uṉṉuvāṉāka.  ippayiṟci makaṉip perunili
yil īrttuk koṇṭē yēkum.
īṇṭu yāṉ cila aḻakup poruḷkaḷik kuṟikkiṟēṉ.  avi tiṅkaḷ, malar, paṟavi, kuḻanti, peṇmi mutaliyaṉa.  ivaṟṟiṉpāl karutti aṭikkaṭi celuttal paranta orumi aḻakik kāṇa aṭikōlvatākum.
peṇṇi māyiyeṉṟum malameṉṟum cila aṟiñar pēciyirukka, nīvir aḻakuk kaṭavuḷik kāṇṭaṟkup peṇṇi vaḻipaṭu poruḷākap pukalvateṉṉi eṉṟu cilar viṉava
lām.  aḻakuk kaṭavuḷik kāṇṭaṟkup peṇṇiṉuñ ciṟanta vaḻipaṭuporuḷ oṉṟilli eṉṟē valiyuṟuttuvēṉ.  peṇṇiluḷḷa aḻakuk kaṭavuḷik kāṇpōṉukkup piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa aḻaku tāṉē pulaṉākum.
peṇmi eṉpatu, eṉpu tōl pōrtta kāmakkūṭu eṉṉum uṇarvuṭiyōr nilikuṟittup peritum iraṅku
kiṟēṉ.  aṉṉār aḻakeṉum muruki uṇartal aritu.  peṇṇiṉ nuṇmiyiyē karutal vēṇṭum.  peṇmiyil tāymiyum iṟimiyum polital uṉṉaṟpālatu.  itu kuṟittup peṇṇiṉ perumi allatu vāḻkkit tuṇi eṉṉum nūṟkaṇ virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷēṉ.  virivu āṇṭuk kāṇka.
ivvulakil taṉittaṉiyāka uṇarntu iṉpuṟattakka aḻakelām tiraṇṭa kaṭavuḷ vaṭivam peṇ vaṭivam eṉka.  peṇṇiṭattuḷḷa kaṭavuḷ aḻakik karutik karuti, naṉṟikku vittākum nalloḻukkatti vaḷarttuvaral vēṇṭum.  peṇṇit tīyoḻukkak kuṟippōṭu nōkkuvatu virivil aḻivataṟkuk kālkoḷvatākum.  aḻakelān tiraṇṭa aḻakuk kaṭavuḷākiya peṇṇi uḷattirutti, vāyāṟ pāṭik kiyāl toḻutu, vaḻipaṭutalāl makkaḷ vāḻviṉ nōkkam niṟivēṟum.  peṇṇik kaṭavuḷākak karutip pōṟṟuva
tiṉuñ ciṟanta tavam piṟitoṉṟirukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṉakkut tōṉṟavilli.  ivvuṇmi tēṟātu, peṇteyvatti māyiyeṉak karuti, vilaṅkiṉpam nukara virumpuvatiṉuṅ koṭiya maṟam vēṟoṉṟuṇṭō?
taṉṉāl aḻakuṭiyateṉak karutappaṭum oṉṟi orumiyuḷaṅ koṇṭu vaḻipāṭṭuvarūum oruvaṉ nāḷaṭi
vil aḻaki yāṇṭuṅ kāṇappeṟuvaṉ.  avaṉukku iyaṟki yulakamē murukaṉāka viḷaṅkum.  annili eytiya oruvaṉiṭam murukaṉ iyalpukaḷāya maṇamum iḷamiyum kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi
yum aḻakum patitalāl avaṉ murukaṉākiṟāṉ.  avaṉukku aḻivētu?
aḻiyāp pēṟu nalkavalla cila vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷic ciṟitu virittuk kūṟap pukukiṟēṉ.

5. pāṭṭum ōviyamum iciyum piṟavum
(iyaṟki vaḻipāṭṭup paṭikaḷ - pāṭṭu, ōviyam, ici - iyaṟkip paṭam pāṭṭu - iḷaṅkō, cēkkiḻār, kampar ōviyaṅkaḷ - tamiḻ nūlkaḷ - tamiḻarkkoru viṇṇappam - kalittokiyum tirukkōviyārum - tirukkōyilum ōviyamum - ōviyamum kāviyamum  - uruva vaḻipāṭṭiṉ nuṭpam - ōviyamum iyaṟkiyum - maliyum kōyilum - ceyaṟki aṇikaḷ - iciyum yōkamum - apparum patañcaliyārum - iciyum iyaṟkiyum - iciyum iṟivaṉum - vaṉṟoṇṭar - ṣēkpiyar - iciyum oḻuṅkum - cēkkiḻār - eṭṭup poruḷ vaḻipāṭu - ñāyiṟu vaḻipāṭu - uṭalōmpal. )
iyaṟki aḻaki uṇarum pēṟu peṟutal eḷitaṉṟu.  aritil muyaṉṟu appēṟu peṟṟa oruvaṉ ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyum ati iḻattalākātu.  appēṟṟiṟku iṭar viḷikkap pala
tiṟap pēykaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa.  appēykaḷiṉ cūḻccikku eḷimi
yāy iriyākātavāṟu makaṉ taṉṉik kāttuk koḷvatē aṟivuṭimi.  itaṟkeṉ ceyvatu?
iyaṟkiyi iṭiyaṟātu makaṉ niṉinta vaṇṇamirut
tal vēṇṭum.  oṉṟi iṭiyīṭiṉṟi niṉintu koṇ
ṭirukkum iyalpu mikac cilarkkē amivatuṇṭu.  maṟṟap perumpāṉmiyōrkku avviyalpu eḷitil amivatilli.  ātalāl iyaṟki niṉiviṉiṉṟum piriyātavāṟu tammik kākkavalla muyaṟcikaḷil ivar talippaṭutal ciṟappu.  im
muyaṟciyi vaḻipāṭu eṉṟuṅ kūṟalām.  vaḻipāṭukaḷ palatiṟat
taṉa.  avaravar tattam āṟṟaluk kiyaṉṟatum, iyalpiṟkup poruntiyatumāka uḷḷa ovvoruvita vaḻipāṭṭik koḷvārāka.
īṇṭuc ciṟappāka muttiṟa vaḻipāṭukaḷik kuṟikkiṟēṉ.  oṉṟu pāṭṭu;  maṟṟoṉṟu ōviyam;  iṉṉoṉṟu ici.  ivi makkaḷ uṇarviyum iyaṟkiyiyun toṭarpup
paṭuttattakka pālaṅkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa.  
mutalāvatu pāṭṭi eṭuttuk koḷvōm.  pāṭṭāva
teṉṉi?  iyaṟkiyē pāṭṭu.  iyaṟki pāṭṭeṉil ataṉ uḷḷuṟiyāka uḷḷa muruku allatu aḻakum pāṭṭākum.  iyaṟkiyi uḷḷavāṟu eḻuttāl aṇipeṟa varivatum pāṭṭē. ippāṭṭi iyaṟkiyiṉ paṭam eṉṟu collalām.
iyaṟki epporuḷi uḷattil patikkiṟatō, ap
poruḷiyē ataṉ paṭamāka uḷḷa pāṭṭum patittal vēṇṭum;  aṅṅaṉam pativiyātatu pāṭṭaṉṟu.
kurumaṇi uṟintu uruṇṭu tiraṇṭāleṉa iruḷ cūḻnta koṇṭal muṭiyum, ceññāyiṟṟiṉ eḻukati rumiḻum iḷaveyilil oṉṟi aḻaku kāṭṭum paccip pacuṅkāṭṭup pōrviyum, maṇi koḻittu muḻavārttuc caṅkolikkum aruvi yaṇiyum, vaṇṭu yāḻmūralum, kuyilpāṭṭum, maññi
yāṭṭuṅ koṇṭa oru mālvari niṟkiṟatu.  avvariyi eḻuttāl ōviyamāka oru pulavaṉ varinta pāṭṭu maṟṟōriṭat
til polikiṟatu.
ammaliyip pārātu ippāṭṭik kaṇṇuṟṟa vēṟoru pulavaṉum, ippāṭṭik kāṇātu ammaliyi nōkkiya maṟṟoru pulavaṉum oru niliyattil cantikkiṉṟaṉar.  muṉṉiyapulavaṉ pāṭṭiṉ ciṟappik kūṟap pukuntāṉ.  pāṭṭup poruḷi avaṉ virittup pakaruṅkāl, poruḷiṉ
pattic cevimaṭutta maṟṟoruvaṉ, iḵteṉṉa pāṭṭā?  maliyā?  vaḻiyiṭi yāṉ kaṇṭa oru maliyippaṟṟip pēcu
kiṟīrē!  ammaliyiṉ paṭamō ippāṭṭu?  m«kiy, òw¤nj tH§»a fh£áiaí« ï‹g¤ijí«, ï¥gh£L mf¤nj tH§F»wnj! eṉṟu makiḻveytiṉāṉ.  piṉṉi avviru pulavaruṅ kuṟitta mali pōntu, ammali pāṭṭākap pāviyuḷḷa aḻaki yuṇarntu iṉpuṟṟaṉar.  iṉpattil iruvarum oruvarāyiṉar.
puṟattē iyaṟkiyik kāṇpōṉ ettaki iṉpatti nukarkiṟāṉō, attaki iṉpatti avviyaṟkip pāṭṭi ōriṭattiliruntu paṭippōṉum nukartal vēṇṭum.  ittaṉmi vāynta oṉṟē pāṭṭākum.
pāṭṭukkuriya poruṭ kāṭciyiyātal, ataṟkuriya iṉpattiyātal vaḻaṅkātatu pāṭṭākātu.  atu veṟuñ coṟkaṭṭēyākum.  atu virivil uluttuppōkum.
iyaṟkiyi uḷḷavāṟu kuṟikkum pāṭṭi aṇipeṟa yākka vallāṉ evaṉ?  pulaṉkaḷi iyaṟki vaḻippaṭuttit tūymi ceytu, iyaṟki yaḻaki uḷattāl mukarntu, uṇarviṟku iṉpaviruntūṭṭum oruvaṉē iyaṟkiyip pāṭṭil vaṭikkavallāṉ.  appulavaṉ iyaṟkiyip pāṭ
ṭōviyamāka variyumpōtu, avaṉ pulaṉkaḷilum, uḷatti
lum, uṇarvilum, iyaṟkiyum, iyaṟkip poruḷuṟuppuk
kaḷum, kūṟupāṭukaḷum, iyalpukaḷum, piṟavum evvāṟu paṭikiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu collalum vēṇṭumō?  avaṉ iyaṟki vaṇṇamākiṟāṉ.
iyaṟki paṭiyum iṭantoṟum aḻakeṉum murukaṉ eḻuntaruḷvaṉ. ākavē iyaṟkiyip pāṭap pukuvatum murukaṉ vaḻipāṭātal kāṇka.  iyaṟkiyip pāṭa iyalātār iyaṟkip pulavar pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷip poruḷuṇarntu ōti, iyaṟki iṉpattil tiḷippārāka.  ituvum murukaṉ vaḻipāṭākum.
ulakil pala iṭaṅkaḷil pala pulavar tam tam moḻiyil iyaṟkiyip pāṭṭōviyamāka eḻutiyirukkiṟār.  nantamiḻp pulavarum iyaṟkip paṭam pala varintu namakku utaviyuḷḷaṉar.  appaṭaṅkaḷ pallāyirak kaṇakkākak kaṭaleṉap perukik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.  akkaṭaliṉiṉṟum iraṇṭoru tuḷi eṭuttuc coṭṭukiṟēṉ.
ticimukam pacantu cemmalark kaṇkaḷ 
muḻunīr vāra muḻumeyum paṉittut
tirinī rāṭi yirunila maṭanti
yaracukeṭut talamvaru mallaṟ kāli
iḵtu iḷaṅkō varinta antivāṉ ōviyam.
nīlamā mañci ēṅka nirikkoṭi puṟavam pāṭak
kōlaveṇ mukiyēr mulli kōpamvāy muṟuval kāṭṭa   
ālumiṉ ṉiṭicūḻ mālip payōtaram aciya vantāḷ
ñālanī ṭaraṅki lāṭak kāreṉum paruva nallāḷ
itu cēkkiḻāratu kārkāla ōviyam.
taṇṭali mayilka ḷāṭat tāmari viḷakkan tāṅkak
koṇṭalkaṇ muḻavi ṉēṅkak kuvaḷikaṇ viḻittu nōkkat
teṇṭiri yeḻiṉi kāṭṭat tēmpiḻi makara yāḻiṉ
vaṇṭuka ḷiṉitu pāṭa marutamvīṟ ṟirukku mātō
puviyiṉuk kaṇiyā yāṉṟa poruṭantu pulattiṟ ṟāki
aviyakat tuṟika ṭāṅki aintiṇi neṟiya ḷāvic
caviyuṟatteḷintu taṇṇeṉ ṟoḻukkamuntaḻuviccāṉṟōr
kaviyeṉat teḷinta kōtā variyiṉi vīrar kaṇṭār
veyyōṉoḷi taṉmēṉiyiṉ viricōtiyiṉ maṟiyap
poyyōveṉum iṭiyāḷoṭum iḷiyāṉoṭum
miyōmara katamōmaṟi kaṭalōmaḻi mukilō
ayyō ivaṉ vaṭiveṉpator aḻiyā aḻakuṭiyāṉ  				pōṉāṉ
veyiliḷa nilavēpōl virikati riṭivīcap
payilmara niḻalīṉap paṉipuri tuḷimēkap
puyaltara viḷameṉkāl pūvaḷa viyateyta
mayiliṉa naṭamāṭum vaḻiyiṉi yaṉapōṉār;
ivi kampariṉ eḻuttōviyaṅkaḷ.
ulakiṭi vāḻnta perumpulavarkaḷ aḻakeṉum murukaṉ vaḻipāṭṭi iṭiyīṭiṉṟi nikaḻttaṟ poruṭṭup pākkaḷāl kāviyaṅkaḷiyum, nāṭakaṅkaḷiyum, vāḻttukkaḷiyum, piṟavaṟṟiyum pāṭiṉārkaḷ.  ippāṭṭut toṇṭāl avarkaḷ iyaṟki vaṭivāṉa iṟivaṉōṭiyinta iṉpa vāḻvi ivvulakilēyē peṟṟārkaḷ.  avarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭṭuk kāviyaṅkaḷ eḻuttukkaḷalla;  coṟkaḷalla;  cīr
kaḷalla;  taḷikaḷalla;  aṭikaḷalla;  toṭikaḷalla.  avikaḷ iyaṟkiyiṉ eḻuttōviyaṅkaḷ.  ulaki nōkkumpōtu tōṉṟum malikaḷiṉ aṇiyum, kāṭukaḷiṉ ceṟivum, ceṭi koṭikaḷiṉ niriyum, pacumpayirp paraviyum, karuṅkaṭalum, ceññāyiṟum, nilavut tiṅkaḷum, nīla vāṉamum, paṟavikaḷiṉ tuḻaṉiyum, vilaṅkukaḷiṉ īṭṭamum, makkaḷiṉ kūṭṭamum, akkūṭṭattiṉ uḻavu vāṇipam āṭci aṟam moḻi vaḻakku oḻukkamum, piṟavum kāviyaṅkaḷiluṅ kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.  ovvoru kāviyamum iyaṟki ulakiṉ ovvoru paṭamākum.
iyaṟkiyil muruku uṟikiṟatu.  iyaṟki pāṭṭām
pōtu, appāṭṭilum muruku uṟikiṟatu.  pāṭṭukkaḷā lākkappeṟuṅ kāviyamō murukaṉ vīṟṟiruntaruḷuṅ kōyi
lākiṟatu.  ikkōyil vaḻipāṭu muṭṭiṉṟi naṭipeṟum iṭaṅ
kaḷil makiḻcci poṅkik koṇṭirukkum.
muṉṉit tamiḻp perum pulavarum pāṭṭuk kāviyap peruṅkōyil pala amittirukkiṟār.  pattuppāṭṭu, eṭṭut
toki, aimperuṅkāppiyam, periyapurāṇam, kamparāmā
yaṇam, tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam, kāñcip purāṇam, tēvāram, tiruvācakam, tiruvāymoḻi, tiruppukaḻ mutaliya tamiḻkkōyilkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl vaḻipāṭu maṭṭum arukiyirukkiṟatu.  ikkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ kimmāṟeṉṉē!  eṉṉē!  arccaṉi pāṭṭēyākum eṉṟu tamiḻk kaṭavuḷ pāṭṭiyē viḻintu niṟkiṟatu.  pāṭṭu vaḻipāṭō aruki niṟ
kiṟatu.  pāṭṭut toṇṭillāta iṭattil aṉpētu?  iṉ
pētu?  makiḻcciyētu?  itaṉāl tamiḻ makkaḷpālum, avarkaḷ vāḻvilum aḻakeṉum murukaṉ āṭutaloḻintāṉ.  mutal mutal iyaṟkiyil murukeṉum aḻakik kaṇṭa tamiḻarkaḷ nili ivvāṟāyiṟṟu!  aḻakik kaṇṭavarkaḷ aḻakiḻantārkaḷ!  tamiḻarkaḷē!  iyaṟki uṅkaḷ celvam;  ataṉ aḻaku uṅkaḷ uṭimi;  avvaḻakup pāṭṭu uṅkaḷ aṇi.  mīṇṭum avi
kaḷip peṟa muyaluṅkaḷ;  nīṅkaḷ paṇṭi nili eytuvīrkaḷ.
uṅkaḷ tamiḻp perum iṉpa niliyaṅkaḷuḷ īṅkuk kalittokiyum tirukkōviyārum eṉṉuḷḷattik kavarkiṉṟaṉa.  avikaḷiṉ eḻuttu, col, cīr, taḷi, aṭi, toṭi eṉṉum ovvoṉṟilum iyaṟkiyum, aḻakum, pāṭṭum maruvukiṉṟaṉa.  avikaḷil yāṇṭum maṇam;  yāṇ
ṭum iḷami;  yāṇṭuṅ kaṭavuḷ taṉmi;  yāṇṭum aḻaku!  eṉṉarumit tamiḻ makkaḷic ciṟappākak kalittoki, tirukkōviyār mutaliya nūlkaḷōṭu uṟavukoṇṭu vāḻumāṟu vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.  peṇṇip pēyiṉpap poru
ḷeṉak karutun tūrttarkaṭkut tirukkōviyār kāma
nūlākat tōṉṟalām.  peṇṇi murukākap pōṟṟuvōrk
kaṉṟō tirukkōviyār iṉpa nūlākat tōṉṟum?
iyaṟki aḻakup poruḷilum tamiḻp pākkaḷilum murukik kāṇa iyalāta cakōtararporuṭṭu, āṅkāṅkē murukapperumāṉukkut tirukkōyilkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruk
kiṉṟaṉa.  attirukkōyil vaḻipāṭṭāṉum pēṟu peṟalām.
tirukkōyil eṉpatu kaṭṭiṭamaṉṟu;  aḵtu anniṉivi
yūṭṭuvatumaṉṟu.  tirukkōyil, iyaṟkiyiyum iyaṟkiyiṉ uḷḷuṟi poruḷiyum - niṉivūṭṭuvatu.  nāṭukaḷil et
tuṇiyō kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷirukkiṉṟaṉa.  ikkaṭṭiṭaṅkaṭkum kōyil kaṭṭiṭaṅkaṭkum vēṟṟumiyuṇṭu.  kōyilkaḷiṉ tali, uṭal, uṟuppu, uḷḷam ellām ōviyaṅkaḷāl aṇi ceyyap paṭṭiruttal kāṇkiṟōm.  maṟṟak kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil ōviyaṅkaḷ amikkappaṭuvatilli.  nam muṉṉōr kōyil
kaḷi ōviyamākkiyataṉ nuṭpatti ōrtal vēṇṭum.  iyaṟki, ataṉ uḷḷuṟi eṉṉum iraṇṭiṉ cāyalkaḷē kōyil
kaḷāyiṉa.  iyaṟkiyum, ataṉi viṭṭup piriyāta ataṉ uḷḷuṟiyām aḻakum kōyilkaḷil palatiṟa ōviyaṅkaḷākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa.
ōviyaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa kāviyaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi.  kāviyaṉ iyaṟkiyik kaviyāka eḻutukiṟāṉ.  ōviyaṉ iyaṟkiyi ōviyamāka varikiṟāṉ.  kāviyamum ōviyamum oru nōkkuṭi
yaṉavē.  karuttil iraṇṭum iyaṟki yaḻakiyē patikkiṉṟaṉa.
kāviyaṉ kaṇṇuṅ karuttum eṅṅaṉam iyaṟkik kūṟu
kaḷil paṭikiṉṟaṉavō, aṅṅaṉē oviyaṉ kaṇṇuṅ karuttum avikaḷil paṭikiṉṟaṉa.  ōviyaṉ iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ iyaṟkiyāy niṉṟē ōviyameḻutal karutaṟpālatu.
iyaṟki vaṭivaṅkaḷip pulaṉkaḷ vaḻi uḷattil niṟutti, uṇarviṟkoṇṭu, avikaḷik kiyāl paṭamākavum vaṭiva
mākavum muṟiyē eḻutalum amittalum evvaḷavu arumi?  kallākavum paḷiṅkākavum piṟavākavum kiṭakkum piṇṭap poruḷilē, ciṟpaṉ uṇarviluḷḷa iyaṟkiyaḻakiṉ nuṇmi vaṭivam, avaṉ kiyiṉvaḻi yiṟaṅki urukkoḷkiṟatu.  ivvuru, tāṉ tikaḻun kalliyum piṟavaṟṟiyum maṟittu iyaṟki
yaḻakip poruttukiṟatu.
ittakiya ōviyatti eṉṉeṉṟu kūṟuvatu?  eṉ
ṉeṉṟu koḷvatu?  ōviyam kallā?  paḷiṅkā?  niṟamā?  ōvi
yam mukiḻttatum kallum maramum paḷiṅkum maṟikiṉṟaṉa.  iyaṟki nuṭpamuṇarāta ceyaṟki mākkaṭkup pulaṉākum poruḷippaṟṟiya ārāycciyē īṇṭu vēṇṭuvatilli.  aṉṉār ceyaṟkiyil toṭakkuṇṭu kiṭappōr.  iyaṟki
nuṭpa muṇarntōrkkē ōviyattiṉ peṟṟi pulaṉākum.  ōviyattik kaṇṭu makiḻntu makiḻntu karutak karuta atu pulaṉ
kaḷukku iṉpūṭṭi iyaṟki aḻakām muruki uṇarttukiṟatu.  ōviyam makaṉik kavarntukoṇṭē iyaṟkiyaḻakil avaṉik kūṭṭukiṟatu.  ivvōviyatti eṉṉeṉṟu koḷ
vatu?  iti iyaṟki yaḻakiṉ cāyal eṉṟu kūṟalām;  allatu iyaṟkiyaḻakiṉ kuḻavi eṉṟu koḷḷalām.
kiyāl ceyyappaṭuvatu ceyaṟki yeṉṟum, kiyāl ceyyappaṭātatu iyaṟkiyeṉṟum muṉṉar ōriṭattil kuṟippiṭṭati īṇṭu aṉparkaṭku niṉivūṭṭukiṟēṉ.  atikkoṇṭu, ōviyamum kipuṉintiyaṟṟum oṉṟākalāṉ, ati īṇṭu ēṟpateṉṉi eṉṟu cila aṉparkaḷ aiyuṟalām.  ōviyam iyaṟkiyaḻakiṉ peṟṟiyi niṉivūṭṭi uṇartta
lāṉum, aḵtu iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ pulaṉkaḷi āṟṟuppaṭutta
lāṉum, iyaṟki vaḻipāṭṭiṟku aḵtu ēṟṟaṟkuriyatē.  ōviya uruppeṟāta kīṟalkaḷum piṟa kōraṅkaḷum pulaṉkaḷi iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ āṟṟuppaṭuttāmiyāṉ, avi ceyaṟkiyiṉ pāṟ paṭuvaṉavām.  ōviyamō pulaṉkaḷi iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ toṭarpuppaṭuttukiṟatu.  itupaṟṟiyē ōviyatti iyaṟki
yaḻakiṉ cāyal eṉṟum, kuḻavi eṉṟuṅ kiḷantu kūṟalāṉēṉ.  aḻakuṟiyum iyaṟki niliyattit tiṟakkavalla tiṟavu eṉṟu vēṟu vaḻipaṭuttiyum ōviyattik kūṟalām.  karutti iyaṟki
yuṭaṉ toṭarpuppaṭuttum ceyaṟkikkum, maṟṟac ceyaṟ
kikkum vēṟṟumiyuṇṭeṉka.  ōviya vaḻipāṭu, iyaṟki vaḻipāṭṭiṉ toṭakka niliyiliruppōrk kuriyateṉṟu mēlē coṟṟatum īṇṭuk karutaṟpāṟṟu.
ōviyaṅkaḷiṉ  perukkē kōyileṉpatu.  kōyili
ṉuḷḷē oḷirum ovvoru vaṭivamum iyaṟkiyaḻakiṉ kuḻaviyeṉum ōviyattiṉpāṟpaṭṭatē.  eṉavē ōviyakkūṭa
māka viḷaṅkuṅ kōyil, iyaṟkiyiṉ taṉmiyiyum, ataṉuṭporuḷiṉ iyalpiyum makkaṭku uṇarttuvateṉka.
innōkkuṭaṉ amikkappeṟṟa ōviya niliyamān tirukkōyil celvōr āṇṭeḻuntaruḷi yuḷḷa poruḷi evvuṇarvuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭalvēṇṭum?
tirukkōyil uḷḷirukkum murukap perumāṉ tiruvuru
vam, maṇam iḷami kaṭavuṭṭaṉmi aḻaku eṉṉum ivi
kaṭku aṟikuṟi eṉṉum uṇarvōṭu (murukaṉi) vaḻipāṭu ceytalvēṇṭum.  ivvuṇarvōṭu ceyyappaṭātatu kal cempu vaḻipāṭāka muṭiyum.  itaṉāl aṟiyāmi nīṅkātu;  aḻakiṉpa aṉpun tōṉṟātu.
tirukkōyi luḷḷirukkun tirumēṉi taṉṉic
civaṉeṉavē kaṇṭavarkkuc civaṉuṟiva ṉaṅkē
eṉavarūum aruṇanticivattiṉ tiruvākkiyōrka.
nam muṉṉōr murukapperumāṉukkup peritum mali
kaḷiṉum maliyiṉ pāṅkariṉumē tirukkōyil kaṭṭiṉar;  ovvoru kuṉṟum murukaṉ tirukkōyileṉa nam muṉṉōr aṟivuṟuttiyuḷḷaṉar.  nakkīraṉār tamatu tirumurukāṟṟup
paṭiyuḷ kuṉṟutōṟāṭalik koṇṭatum uṉṉattakkatu.  murukaṉ vaḻipāṭṭic ceyaṟkik kōyilaḷavōṭu nammavar niṟuttiviṭap pōkiṟāreṉṟu añci, nantamiḻc cāṉṟōr iyaṟkik kōyilākiya maliyiyuṅ kōyileṉak koḷḷu
māṟu namakku aṟivuṟuttiṉar.  murukaṉi maliyāka vaḻipaṭiṉ, piṉṉi ammali peru ulakamāki murukaṉ uṇmi vaṭivip pulappaṭuttum.
murukaṉ, iyaṟkiyaḻakuk kaṭavuḷ eṉṟa uṇarvilāc cilar, murukaṉ tiruvuruvic ceyaṟki aṇikaḷāl aḻaku cey
kiṟār.  iyaṟki aḻakaṉic ceyaṟki aṇiyā aḻaku cey
yum?  tiruviḻā tiruviḻāveṉac ceyaṟkit tīneṟi paṟṟit tiripavarkkup peritum iraṅkukiṟēṉ.  murukaṉ viḻākka
ḷellām iyaṟki aḻakuṇarviyūṭṭumāṟu koṇṭāṭap peṟalvēṇṭum.  illiyēl viḻākkaḷiṉ poruḷ iṟantu
paṭum.
iṉi ici vaḻipāṭṭiṉmītu karuttic celuttuvōm.  ici vaḻipāṭṭi yōka vaḻipāṭeṉṟuṅ kūṟalām.  ici
yiṉ payaṉ etuvō atuvē yōkattiṉ payaṉum.  yōka
māvatu, pulaṉkaḷ orumippaṭṭuk karuvi karaṇak kuṟumpukaḷi oṭukkik kūṭṭum amitiyil irumi uṇarvukeṭa, aṟiñar akamukattil nikaḻttum oru vaḻipāṭu.  oṉṟiyiruntu niṉi
miṉkaḷ untamakku ūṉamilli eṉṟār apperamperu
māṉārum.  yōka;  citta vrutti nirōta: eṉap patañcaliyār yōkam eṉṉuñ coṟkup poruḷ virittiruttali nōkkuka.  inniliyi iciyuṅ kūṭṭutalāl, iciyiṉ payaṉiyum yōkattiṉ payaṉiyum oṉṟeṉa urikkiṟēṉ.
iciñāṉiyic ciṟanta yōki eṉṟu yāṉ colvēṉ.  īṇṭu yōkam yōkam eṉṟu colvatu, piṟavaṟṟil kaṭavuḷ illiyeṉṟum, tampāl maṭṭuṅ kaṭavuḷuṇṭu eṉṟuṅ karuti, ellāvaṟṟiyun tuṟantu, vaḻi tuṟiyiṉṟi, mūccip piṭittup pulaṉkaḷik keṭuttu, uṭal nalaṉukkuk kēṭucūḻac cilar koḷḷum pōli yōkatti yaṉṟeṉka.  poruḷillāta yōkamuṟikaḷ pala iṭikkālattil pukuntu viṭṭaṉa.  pulaṉkaḷiṉ tiruviḷiyāṭalkaḷ vaḻiyuḻalātu, neñci nilipeṟutti, amitiyil niṉṟu, oṉṟil oṉṟi, irumiyaṟa ati (avvoṉṟi) uṉṉuvatu yōkam.  inniliyi ovvoruvarum avaravar iyalpiṟkuriya muṟiyil aṭital kūṭum.  pulaṉkaḷum karuvi karaṇaṅkaḷum oṉṟumnili kūṭṭum iyalpu icikkumuṇṭu.  ākavē icivaḻipāṭuñ ciṟanta oṉṟeṉka.
 tiriyaṟṟa nīrpōl neñciṉit teḷivupaṭuttik
koṇṭu, amiti keḻumiya ōriṭattamarntu, ipparanta viyakatti uṟṟunōkkum oruvaṉukku ellām icimaya
mākap pulaṉākum.  annōkkil nilittut tiḷippat tiḷippa, avaṉ taṉṉil avvici muḻaṅkuvatuṇarvaṉ;  mēlum mēlum avaṉ anniliyil paṭiyappaṭiya, ellā
vaṟṟiṟkum mutalākavuḷḷa aḻakupporuḷ icimayamā yiruttaliyum uṇarvaṉ.
ellām valla meypporuḷ ulaku uyirkaḷil poruntiyirukkiṟatu eṉṟu collappaṭukiṟatu.  eṅṅaṉam apporuḷ porunti yirukkiṟateṉil, aḵtu avaṟṟuḷ icipaṭap poruntiyirukkiṟateṉka.  ulaku uyir kaṭavuḷ mūṉ
ṟum iciyil piṇikkappaṭṭuk kalantu niṟkiṉṟaṉa.  ikkalappē attuvita niliyeṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟupa.  attuvita niliyum ici paranta oṉṟākum.  ellāvaṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāyuḷḷa aḻakupporuḷ icimayamā yirukkiṟateṉil, maṟṟavikaḷi ici mayameṉṟu collavēṇṭuvatilli.  āṇṭavaṉ icimayattaṉ eṉṉum uṇmiyi nan tamiḻp periyōrkaḷ uṇarntu ulakiṟku aṟivuṟutti yirukkiṟārkaḷ.
ēḻiciyāy icippayaṉāy eṉṉuṭiya tōḻaṉumāy
eṉṟu vaṉṟoṇṭar tamatu tōḻarāṅ kaṭavuḷi vaḻutti
yiruttal kāṇka.
iciyā yiruppavaṉum iṟivaṉ;  icippayaṉā yiruppavaṉum avaṉ.  eṉṉē iciyiṉ māṇpu!  ici
vaṇṇamāyuḷḷa oṉṟi iciyāl eḷitil peṟalāmaṉṟō?  ākavē ici vaḻipāṭum iṟivaṉukku iṉiyatoṉṟākum.
iciyiṉ iyalpukaḷ palappala.  avikaḷuḷ oṉṟu atāvatu taliyāyatu paṉmiyi orumip paṭuttuvatu.  iciyil iṉpam maruvutaṟkuṅ kāraṇamituvē.  pulaṉkaḷ orumiyuṟṟuk karuvi karaṇaṅkaḷiṉ koṭṭattik kulikku
miṭattil eḻuvatu ici.  maṟṟa iṭattil eḻuvatu vaci.  ici pāṭap pukuvōṉuṭiya pulaṉkaḷ paṉmiyuṇar viḻantu orumiyuṇarvu peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  iciyic cevimaṭuppōṉum annili eytukiṟāṉ.  poṟipulaṉ karuvi karaṇac cēṭṭi
kaḷiṉiṉṟum makaṉi ici viṭutali ceykiṟatu.  avikaḷi
ṉiṉṟum viṭutaliyaṭiyum oruvaṉ piṉṉi etaṉuṭaṉ icivaṉ?  avaṉ iṉpa aṉpup poruḷām aḻakuṭaṉ icivaṉ.  uyir cārntataṉ vaṇṇamā yiruppatoṉṟākaliṉ, aḵtu oṉṟi viṭuttatum maṟṟoṉṟip paṟṟalvēṇṭu maṉṟō?
ivvāṟu iciñāṉi cēṭṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭutali
yaṭintu, iṉpa aṉpil icitalāl, aṉpi avaṉ evvuyiriṉuṅ kāṇkiṟāṉ.  innili eytum oruvaṉ ev
vuyirkkātal tīṅku ceyvaṉō?  ceyyāṉ.  evvuyiriṭattum irakkaṅkoḷḷum oru perun taṉmiyi ici kūṭṭukiṟatu.
oruvaṉ ulakil icip pulavaṉā yiruttal vēṇṭum.  allatu icikēṭṭu urukum uḷattaṉā yiruttal vēṇṭum.  iraṇṭum peṟāta oruvaṉ makkaḷ iṉattil cērntavaṉallaṉ.  avaṉāl ici vaṇṇamāyuḷḷa maṉpatikkē kēṭu uṇ
ṭākum.  ici iṉimi uṇarātavaṉ koliñaṉē yāvaṉ.  ivvāṟē ṣēkpiyar eṉṉuṅ kavimaṉṉar karutukiṟār.  ātalāl, makkaḷ iṉpa vāḻviṟku ici iṉṟiyamiyāta teṉka.
ici iyaṟkiyil parantu niṟkiṟatu.  ellām icimaya
meṉṟu mēlē collappaṭṭatu.  muṉivarar pōla aṭaṅkit talicāyttu niṟkuñ ceñcālikaḷ meṉkāṟṟil oṉṟō
ṭoṉṟu koñcik koñci ici pāṭukiṉṟaṉa.  vāṉuṟa
vōṅki vaḷampeṟa vaḷarntu maḻi tavaḻum mūṅkilkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu aḷavaḷāvi ici pāṭukiṉṟaṉa.  vaṇṭukaḷ pāṇ miḻaṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.  valampurikaḷ caṅkītam muḻakkukiṉṟaṉa.  paṟavikaḷiṉ pāṭṭoliyi eṉṉeṉṟu pakarvēṉ!  aḻakiya vāṉampāṭi!  uṉpāṭṭi eṉṉeṉpēṉ!  vāṉattil nī ivarumpōtum, iḻiyum pōtum uṉṉiciyum, muṟiyē ivarntum (ārōkaṇam) iḻintum (avarōkaṇam) ulakiṟ
kūṭṭa uṉakku evarē kaṟpittār?  kītamiṉiya kuyilē uṉpāl iṉimiyi evarē amittār?  kuḻantikaḷiṉ maḻali icikku nikaruṇṭō?  ā!  ā!  peṇ teyvaṅkaḷiṉ moḻiyō paṇmayam!  paṇmayam!  ivvāṟu ici iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ paṭarntu niṟkiṟatu.  ivviciyuṭaṉ icivatē pāṇaṉ iciyum.
makkaḷ aṉivarum pāṇarākār.  makkaḷ oli yellām iciyākā.  eṉṉi?  iyaṟkiyil ici oḻuṅkupaṭṭē niṟ
kiṟatu.  iyaṟkiyiṉ oḻuṅkum, ataṉuṭaṉ maruvum iciyiṉ oḻuṅkuṅ karutaṟpālaṉa.  oḻuṅkupaṭṭa vāḻvuṭiyāṉ iyaṟkivaḻi niṟpavaṉākiṟāṉ.  avaṉ vāḻvu iciyuṭiya
teṉka.  oḻuṅkupaṭṭa vāḻvil, oḻuṅkupaṭṭa naṭiyil, oḻuṅkupaṭṭa pēccil ici cuvikkum.   oḻuṅku paṟṟi, niri niriyē, orēvitamāka naṭakkum pōr vīrar aṇiyil icimaṇakkum.  oḻuṅkupaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷilellām ici
yuṇṭu eṉṟu curuṅkac collalām.  oḻuṅki viṭuttu nīṅkāta ici, ovvoṉṟiluṅ koṇṭuḷḷa uṟavum aḻakiṉpāṟ paṭṭateṉka.
iyaṟki oḻuṅkil oḷirum ici vaṇṇamāyuḷḷa aḻaki allatu muruki uṇara ulakil oḻuṅkici vēṇṭaṟ
pālatu.  iyaṟki oḻuṅku paṟṟiyē nam muṉṉōr ici oḻuṅku vakuttaṉar.  atuvē iciyiyal eṉpatu.  muttamiḻuḷ iciyum oṉṟāy aḻaku ceytal īṇṭuk karutaṟpālatu.  oḻuṅkici miṭaṟṟiṉ vāyilākavum, narappuk karuvikaḷ vāyi
lākavum veḷippōntu, iṉpam payakkum muṟikaḷ aṟiña
rāl kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
oḻuṅkilā ici vaciyēyākum.  oḻuṅkukku uṭ
paṭṭatē iciyākum.  pulaṉkaḷum karuvi karaṇaṅkaḷum oḻuṅku
paṭṭavaḻi eḻuvatu ici eṉṟu mēlē kūṟappaṭṭatum īṇṭu uṉṉattakkatu.
oḻuṅkupaṭṭa ici taṉṉip pāṭuvōriyuñ cevi
maṭuppōriyum iṉpap pēṟṟiṟkuriya orumi nili eytac ceytalāṉ, avvicippāṭum iṟi vaḻipāṭeṉṟu koḷ
vatu poruttamēyākum.  ulakil ici vaḻipāṭu perukap peruka (ulakil) aṉpum iṉpum perukum.
icivaḻi āṇṭavaṉi vaḻuttip pēṟupeṟṟōr palar.  avaruḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭup periyōr oruvari īṇṭu niṉikki
ṟēṉ.  avvaṉpar kuḻalil ici pāṭi iṟimip pēṟu peṟṟa
var.  avaratu ici, evvev vaḻiyil paṭarntu, ulakilum uyirilum ūṭuruvip pāyntu, muṭivil ellāvaṟṟiyuṅ kaṭanta muḻu mutaṟ poruḷik kavarntatu eṉpatic cēkkiḻār perumāṉ cevviya tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷāka icittuḷḷār.  appāṭalkaḷil cila varumāṟu :-
vaḷḷalār vācikku maṇittuḷivāy vēyṅkuḻaliṉ
uḷḷuṟiyañ ceḻuttāka oḻukiyeḻu maturavoli
veḷḷaniṟin tevvuyirkku mēlēmarar taruviḷitēṉ
teḷḷamuti ṉuṭaṉkalantu cevivārppa teṉattēkka
āṉirika ḷaṟukarunti aciviṭā taṇintayarap
pāṉurivāyt tāymuliyiṟ paṟṟumiḷaṅ kaṉṟiṉamum
tāṉuṇavu maṟantoḻiyat taṭamaruppiṉ viṭikkulamum
māṉmutalāṅ kāṉvilaṅku mayirmukiḻttu vantaṇiya
āṭumayi liṉaṅkaḷumaṅ kacivayarntu maruṅkaṇuka
ūṭucevi yiciniṟinta uḷḷamoṭu puḷḷiṉamum 
māṭupaṭin tuṇarvoḻiya maruṅkutoḻil purintoḻukuṅ
kūṭiyavaṇ kōvalaruṅ kuṟiviṉiyiṉ ṟuṟiniṉṟār
nalivārum melivārum uṇarvoṉṟā nayattaliṉāṉ
malivāyveḷ ḷeyiṟṟarava mayiṉmītu maruṇṭuviḻum
caliyāta niliyariyun taṭaṅkariyu muṭaṉcārum
pulivāyiṉ maruṅkaṇiyum pulvāya pulvāyum
maruviyakāl licittaciyā maraṅkaṇmalarc ciṉicaliyā
karuvarivī ḻaruvikaḷuṅ kāṉyāṟuṅ kalittōṭā
perumukiliṉ kulaṅkaḷpuṭi peyarvoḻiyap puṉalcōrā
iruvicumpi ṉiṭimuḻaṅkā eḻukaṭalu miṭituḷumpā
ivvāṟu niṟpaṉavuñ carippaṉavu micimayamāy
meyvāḻum pulaṉkaraṇa mēviyavoṉ ṟāyiṉavāṉ
moyvāca naṟuṅkoṉṟi muṭiccaṭiyā raṭittoṇṭar 
cevvāyiṉ micivitta tirukkuḻalvā caṉiyurukka
meyyaṉpar maṉattaṉpiṉ viḷittalicik kuḻalōci
viyantaṉ ṉiyuniṟittu vāṉantaṉ mayamākkip
poyyaṉpuk keṭṭāta poṟpotuvil naṭampuriyum
aiyaṉṟaṉ tirucceviyiṉ arukaṇiyap perukiyatāl
aṉparkaḷē!  icivaḻipāṭṭiṉ perumiyi nōkkuṅ
kaḷ;  ippāṭalkaḷip pārttup pārttu makiḻuṅkaḷ.  nili
yiyaṟ poruḷiyum iyaṅkiyaṟ poruḷiyum oṉṟupaṭuttip piṉṉit taṉmayamāyuḷḷa āṇṭavaṉpāl ici aṇi
kiṟatu.  itaṉ nuṭpa meṉṉi?  iciyāḷaṉukku ellāt tattuvaṅkaḷum maṟintu, aviyellāñ civamākap poliva
teṉka.  ipperunili kūṭṭum icippāṭṭiyum oruvita vaḻipāṭākak koḷḷalāmaṉṟō?
ivvaḻipāṭukaḷallāta vēṟu vaḻipāṭukaḷum iruk
kiṉṟaṉa.  vaḻipāṭu eṉpatu avaravar aṉpunili aṟivāṟṟal mutaliyavaṟṟip poṟuttu amivatu.  avaravar tattam nilik
kēṟṟavāṟu ovvoruvita vaḻipāṭṭik koḷḷalām.  ellā vaḻipāṭukaḷiṉ muṭivum oṉṟē eṉpati maṭṭum evarum maṟattalākātu.
iṉṉum oru vaḻipāṭṭik kuṟippiṭa virumpukiṟēṉ.  iyaṟki vaḻipāṭukaḷuḷ atu ciṟanta oṉṟākaliṉ, ati iṟutiyil kuṟippiṭukiṟēṉ.  ēṟakkuṟiya ellā vaḻipāṭukaḷum ataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.  avvaḻipāṭāvatu iṟivaṉi eṭṭu vaṭivamākap pōṟṟuvatu.  avveṭṭāvaṉa;  nilam nīr neruppu kāṟṟu veḷi tiṅkaḷ ñāyiṟu uyir eṉpaṉa.  ivi ovvoṉ
ṟiyuṅ taṉittaṉi vaḻipaṭalumuṇṭu.  ivaṟṟi iraṇṭākavō mūṉṟākavō nāṉkākavō pirittu vaḻipaṭalumuṇṭu.  ellā
vaṟṟiyuñ cēravittu vaḻipaṭalumuṇṭu.  ivveṭṭukkum aṭippaṭiyāka niṟkum aṟivup piḻampāṉa aḻakup poru
ḷoṉṟē.

6. murukik kāṇa  muṉippiyaṟuttal 
(muṉippukkūṭātu - cūraṉ pōr - purāṇa  nuṭpam - uruvaka nuṭpam - piṟkālap purāṇikam - pōrkkaḷam - cūraṉ, muruku ivikaḷiṉ uṭporuḷ - muṉippuḷḷavari muruki uṇaral iyalātu - irakkamum murukaṉum - ñāṉacaktiyē murukaṉ vēl - murukaṉatu aruḷ virivu.)
murukaṉi vaḻipaṭap pukuvōriṭam muṉippuṟutal kūṭātu.  iyaṟkiyi luṟiyum muruku muṉippuṭiyōrkkup pulaṉātalaritu.  muṉippum, ataṉ kāṉmuḷikaḷākiya aḻuk
kāṟu avā vekuḷi mutaliyaṉavum, makaṉi nari tiri mūppuk
kāḷākki, muṭivil avaṉic cākkāṭṭuk kiriyākkukiṉṟaṉa.  peṟutaṟkariya makkaṭ piṟaviyip peṟṟum,  atip piṇimūppu cākkāṭṭuk kiriyākkutal aṟivuṭimi yākātu.  muṉippu makkaḷik kollum peruṅkālaṉ.  muṉippuṭiyār murukaṉi eṅṅaṉam uṇartalkūṭum?
kantapurāṇattuḷḷē cūrapaṉmaṉ pōr collap paṭṭirukkiṟatu.  aḵtu iṅkē niṉiviṟku varukiṟatu.  cūraṉ muṉippāl pala vaṭivan tāṅki entiyuṭaṉ perumpōr purin
tāṉ.  muṭivil oṉṟumiyalātu ellāmiḻantu, avaṉ taṉṉan taṉiyaṉāy niṉṟa vēḷi, avaṉatu muṉippuc ciṟitu nēram oṭuṅki otuṅki niṉṟatu.  annērattil yāṇṭumuḷa muruku ava
ṉukkup pulaṉāyiṟṟu.  mīṇṭum muṉippu māṉameṉum vaṭi
vuṟṟu aṭarntatum, murukik kāṇātu, pakimi yuḷaṅ
koṇṭu, avaṉ pōr purintāṉ.
aṇṇalār kumaraṉ mēṉi aṭimutaṉ muṭiviṉ kāṟum
eṇṇilā ūḻi kālam ettiṟa nōkki ṉālum
kaṇṇiṉā laṭaṅkā tuṉṉiṟ karuttiṉā laṭaṅkā teṉpāl
naṇṇiṉā ṉamaruk keṉki aruḷeṉa nāṭṭa lāmē
pōyiṉa akanti pōtam pukuntaṉa valatta tāṉa
tūyatōr tōḷuṅ kaṇṇun tuṭittaṉa puvaṉa meṅku
mēyiṉa poruḷkaṇ muṟṟum veḷippaṭu kiṉṟa viṇṇōr 
nāyakaṉ vaṭivaṅ kaṇṭēṉ naṟṟavap payaṉī taṉṟō
cūḻutal vēṇṭun tāḷkaḷ toḻutiṭal vēṇṭu maṅki
tāḻutal vēṇṭuñ ceṉṉi tutittiṭal vēṇṭun tālu
āḻutal vēṇṭun tīmi akaṉṟunāṉ ivaṟkā ḷāki
vāḻutal vēṇṭum neñcam taṭuttatu māṉa moṉṟē
oṉṉalar poruṭṭā lēki uṟucama riḻitta cemmal
taṉṉuru  vataṉik kāṇkiṉ muṉivatē takuti yākum
vaṉṉikoḷ veṇṇe yēpōl valiyaḻin turukiṟ ṟeṉṟāl
eṉṉuṭi vayatta vaṉṟō uṇarcciyu miyākki muṟṟum
eṉa varūuṅ kantapurāṇap pāṭalkaḷi nōkkuka
ippurāṇa nuṭpatti īṇṭuc ciṟitu ārāyvōmāka.  purāṇattil pōr muḻakkam perumitamākap pēcappaṭukiṟatu;  vīraccuviyum āṅkāṅkē poṅki vaḻikiṟatu.  palatiṟap pōrk karuvikaḷ miṉṉukiṉṟaṉa.  eṉṉē pōriṉ kāṭci!  ivi yāvum uruvakaṅkaḷ!
uruvakaṅkaṭkuriya poruḷi nūṟkaṇ yāṇṭāyiṉum āciriyar pukuttiyiruppar.  aṇikaḷiṉ (alaṅkāra) iṉpatti nukarum aṟiñar, avikaṭkuriya poruḷiyuṇarntum iṉ
puṟuvar.  aṇiyiyal aḷavōṭu niṉṟu payaṉ peṟuvōrip
paṟṟi īṇṭuk karuta vēṇṭuvatilli.  muṉṉināḷ kāviyar uvamameṉṉum uṭalil, poruḷeṉṉum uyiriyum pukut
tum muṟiyik kaṭippiṭittu niṉṟaṉar.  piṉṉi nāḷil tōṉṟiya purāṇikam veṟum uruvakaṅkaḷil (aviyuñ cuviyaṟṟa pōlikaḷil) karuttic celuttik kāviya ulakiṟkē kēṭu cūḻntatu.  ammuṉṉi nūlkaḷiyum, ippiṉṉi nūl
kaḷiyum pakuttaṟiyavēṇṭuvatu ārāyccittuṟi naṇṇu
vōrkaṭaṉ.
cūraṉ pōril aṟiyak kiṭappateṉṉi?  cūraṉ pōr nuṭpam purāṇattilēyē aṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṟatu.  ovvōr uḷḷamum pōrkkaḷam.  akkaḷattil nāṭōṟum cūraṉukkum murukaṉukkum pōr naṭantavaṇṇamirukkiṟatu.  cūraṉ muṉippu;  murukaṉ aruṭkaṭavuḷ.
ovvōr uyiriṭattum muṉippu uṇṭu.  iti nikaḻcciyil uṇarkiṟōm.  muṉippāl viḷivatu naṉ
miyā?  tīmiyā?  tīmi eṉpatu veḷḷiṭimali.  tīmikku mutalākavuḷḷa muṉippik kaḷiyavēṇṭuvatu uyiriṉ kaṭamiyaṉṟō?
uyir koṭiya muṉippāl viḻuṅkappaṭ ṭuṇmiyāṉ, aḵtu akkaṭaṉāṟṟa iyalātukiṭakkiṟatu.  āṉāl uyiruk
kuyirāyuḷḷa aruṭkaṭavuḷ akkaṭaṉāṟṟi varukiṟatu.  uyiruk
kuyirāyk kaṭavuḷ yāṇṭirukkiṟatu eṉṟu cilar niṉikkalām.
piṟarkkuk kēṭu cūḻa muṉippeḻuvatupōlap piṟarkku nalaṉ ceyya ētāvatoṉ ṟuṟukiṟatā illiyā eṉpati uṉṉuvōmāka.  oṉṟuṟuvati uṇarkiṟōm.  aḵteṉṉi?  atuvē irakkam eṉpatu.  irakka nikaḻcci illi eṉṟu evaruñ collār.  irakkam, uṟuvatāy nikaḻvatāy uṇarva
tāyiruppatu, irakkam paṇpu.  ippaṇpiṉ paṇpiyāyiruppatu kaṭavuḷ.  paṇpiyiṉṟip paṇpilli yeṉpatu iyaṟki aṟam.  paṇpiyuṭiya poruḷ paṇpi.  paṇpākiya irakkam perukap perukap paṇpiyākiya iṟiyuṇmiyi yuṇaralām.
muṉippu, uyirit taṉpakkam iruttivikkiṟatu.  uyiri muṉippiṉiṉṟum mīṭka āṇṭavaṉ aruḷ pukukiṟatu.  iraṇṭiṟkum pōr nikaḻkiṟatu.  ippōrē cūraṉ - murukaṉ pōreṉka.
muṉippu eppoḻutu aḻintupaṭum?  tiruvaruḷ patiyap patiya aṟivāṟṟal atāvatu ñāṉacakti eḻum.  aḵteḻuntu pāyumpōtu muṉippu aḻintupaṭum.  ñāṉacaktiyē murukaṉ vēleṉpatu.
cūraṉ muṉippāl, muṉintu pōr purintapōtu, yāṇ
ṭum murukaṉik kaṇṭāṉilli.  ammuṉip poṭuṅkiya
pōtu avaṉ yāṇṭum murukaṉik kāṇum pēṟu peṟṟāṉ.  mīṇṭum ammuṉippuc ciṟitu tali kāṭṭiyatum, avaṉ appēṟṟi iḻantāṉ.  muṉippu, kaṭavuḷippaṟṟiya uṇar
viyum eṇṇattiyun takintu maṟikkum nīrmiyatu.  atuvē iṟappit tantu, piṟappeṉṉum pētimikku vit
tāka niṟpatu.  ātalāl muṉippām arakkaṉik koṉṟu, piṇimūppu cākkāṭoḻittu, iyaṟki maṇamum, māṟā iḷamiyum, kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyum, aḻiyā aḻakumuṭiya murukaṉip peṟa muyalalvēṇṭum.

	viṉiyaṉē ṉēteṉap pukalkēṉ
